import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
@Composable
internal fun PreviewPanel(
    channel: Channel?,
    isFavorite: Boolean,
    onPlay: () -> Unit,
    onToggleFavorite: () -> Unit,
    onGuide: (Channel) -> Unit,
    showEpg: Boolean = true,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val repo = container.repo
    val context = LocalContext.current

    var previewExo by remember { mutableStateOf<ExoPlayer?>(null) }
    var isScreenActive by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

    var isPlaying by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var buffering by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var videoReady by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var muted by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var formatAttempt by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var retryNonce by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var previewError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var tracks by remember { mutableStateOf(Tracks.EMPTY) }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            SahabPlayer.releasePlayer(previewExo)
            previewExo = null
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(previewExo) {
        val exo = previewExo
        if (exo == null) {
            isPlaying = false
            buffering = false
            videoReady = false
            tracks = Tracks.EMPTY
            return@DisposableEffect onDispose { }
        }
        exo.volume = if (muted) 0f else 1f
        val listener = object : Player.Listener {
            override fun onIsPlayingChanged(playing: Boolean) {
                isPlaying = playing
            }

            override fun onPlaybackStateChanged(state: Int) {
                buffering = state == Player.STATE_BUFFERING
            }

            override fun onRenderedFirstFrame() {
                videoReady = true
                previewError = null
            }

            override fun onTracksChanged(newTracks: Tracks) {
                tracks = newTracks
            }

            override fun onPlayerError(error: PlaybackException) {
                if (formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size) {
                    formatAttempt += 1
                } else {
                    buffering = false
                    videoReady = false
                    previewError = "تعذّر تشغيل معاينة البث المباشر (${error.errorCodeName})"
                }
            }
        }
        exo.addListener(listener)
        onDispose {
            exo.removeListener(listener)
        }
    }

    LifecycleEventEffect(Lifecycle.Event.ON_START) {
        isScreenActive = true
    }

    LifecycleEventEffect(Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP) {
        isScreenActive = false
        SahabPlayer.releasePlayer(previewExo)
        previewExo = null
    }

    LaunchedEffect(channel?.id) {
        formatAttempt = 0
        videoReady = false
        previewError = null
        tracks = Tracks.EMPTY
    }

    LaunchedEffect(channel?.id, formatAttempt, isScreenActive, retryNonce) {
        val ch = channel
        if (ch == null || !isScreenActive) {
            previewExo?.stop()
            previewExo?.clearMediaItems()
            if (ch == null) {
                SahabPlayer.releasePlayer(previewExo)
                previewExo = null
            }
            videoReady = false
            previewError = null
            return@LaunchedEffect
        }
        previewError = null
        val url = runCatching {
            repo.streamUrl(PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, ch.id, ch.name, icon = ch.icon, url = ch.url), formatAttempt)
        }.getOrNull()
        if (url.isNullOrBlank()) {
            buffering = false
            videoReady = false
            previewError = "تعذّر تجهيز رابط البث للمعاينة"
            return@LaunchedEffect
        }

        val exo = previewExo ?: SahabPlayer.create(
            context = context,
            client = container.client,
            isLive = true,
            isPreview = true,
            accountHash = container.accountHash,
        ).also { created ->
            previewExo = created
            SahabPlayer.bindOnRelease(created) {
                if (previewExo === created) {
                    previewExo = null
                }
            }
        }

        exo.volume = if (muted) 0f else 1f
        exo.setMediaItem(MediaItem.fromUri(url))
        exo.prepare()
        exo.playWhenReady = true
    }

    // LIVE-01: recover the preview from silent hardware-decoder stalls as well.
    com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player.LiveVideoStallWatchdog(
        player = previewExo,
        enabled = isScreenActive && channel != null,
        itemKey = channel?.id,
        onEscalate = {
            if (formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size) {
                formatAttempt += 1
                true
            } else {
                false
            }
        },
    )

    val openFullScreen: () -> Unit = {
        // Fully release preview player before opening full-screen player (prevents dual MediaCodec/RAM usage)
        SahabPlayer.releasePlayer(previewExo)
        previewExo = null
        onPlay()
    }

    val programs by produceState<List<Program>>(emptyList(), channel?.id) {
        value = if (channel != null && channel.epgId.isNotBlank()) {
            try { repo.shortEpg(channel.id, 6) } catch (_: Exception) { emptyList() }
        } else emptyList()
    }
    val today = formatNowMillis(rememberNow(), "EEEE، d MMMM", Locale.forLanguageTag("ar"))

    Column(
        modifier = modifier,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(14)),
    ) {
        // Card 1: Live Video Preview & Control Toolbar
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(appDp(24)),
            padding = appDp(12),
        ) {
            var previewFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .aspectRatio(16f / 9f)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(appDp(16)))
                    .background(Color.Black)
                    .onFocusChanged { previewFocused = it.isFocused }
                    .border(
                        width = if (previewFocused) appDp(2.5f) else appDp(1),
                        color = if (previewFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(appDp(16)),
                    )
                    .clickable(enabled = channel != null, onClick = openFullScreen),
            ) {
                AndroidView(
                    factory = { ctx ->
                        PlayerView(ctx).apply {
                            useController = false
                            resizeMode = AspectRatioFrameLayout.RESIZE_MODE_ZOOM
                            setShowBuffering(PlayerView.SHOW_BUFFERING_NEVER)
                            layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                            )
                            player = previewExo
                        }
                    },
                    update = { view ->
                        if (view.player !== previewExo) {
                            view.player = previewExo
                        }
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                )

                if (!videoReady) {
                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .background(PreviewPlaceholderBrush),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    ) {
                        if (channel != null) {
                            val primaryPreviewLogo = remember(channel.icon, channel.name) {
                                ChannelLogoResolver.resolvePrimaryUrl(channel.icon, channel.name)
                            }
                            val fallbackPreviewLogo = remember(channel.name) {
                                ChannelLogoResolver.resolveInternetLogoUrl(channel.name)
                            }
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(appDp(64))
                                    .clip(CircleShape)
                                    .background(Color.White)
                                    .border(appDp(2), Palette.FocusCyan, CircleShape)
                                    .padding(appDp(6)),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                            ) {
                                RemoteImage(
                                    url = primaryPreviewLogo,
                                    fallbackUrl = fallbackPreviewLogo,
                                    fallbackText = channel.name,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().clip(CircleShape),
                                    contentScale = ContentScale.Fit,
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(PreviewShadeBrush),
                )

                if (channel == null) {
                    Column(
                        Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.Center)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(appDp(14)))
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.65f))
                            .border(appDp(1), Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(appDp(14)))
                            .padding(horizontal = appDp(16), vertical = appDp(10)),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(6)),
                    ) {
                        Icon(Ic.Play, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(appDp(26)))
                        Text(
                            "اختر قناة واضغط موافق أو المسها للمعاينة",
                            fontSize = appSp(12),
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = Color.White,
                        )
                    }
                } else if (previewError != null) {
                    Column(
                        Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.Center)
                            .padding(horizontal = appDp(14))
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(appDp(14)))
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.80f))
                            .border(appDp(1), Palette.Danger.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(appDp(14)))
                            .padding(horizontal = appDp(16), vertical = appDp(12)),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(8)),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            previewError ?: "تعذّر تشغيل المعاينة",
                            fontSize = appSp(12),
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = Color.White,
                        )
                        GlassButton(
                            text = Ar.retry,
                            onClick = {
                                previewError = null
                                formatAttempt = 0
                                retryNonce += 1
                            },
                            primary = true,
                        )
                    }
                } else if (buffering && !videoReady) {
                    Box(Modifier.align(Alignment.Center)) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(
                            color = Palette.Glow,
                            trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                            modifier = Modifier.size(appDp(32)),
                            strokeWidth = appDp(3),
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Top-right badge: "مباشر - 1080p 60fps"
                Row(
                    Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                        .padding(appDp(10))
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(appDp(8)))
                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.70f))
                        .border(appDp(1), Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f), RoundedCornerShape(appDp(8)))
                        .padding(horizontal = appDp(10), vertical = appDp(5)),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(6)),
                ) {
                    Box(Modifier.size(appDp(7)).clip(CircleShape).background(Palette.Live))
                    val vBadge = SahabPlayer.videoBadge(tracks, channel?.let { qualityOf(it.name) })
                    Text("${Ar.liveBadge} - $vBadge", fontSize = appSp(11), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                }

                // Bottom-left badge: Audio stream info or channel name
                if (channel != null) {
                    val aBadge = SahabPlayer.audioBadge(tracks) ?: channel.name
                    Row(
                        Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                            .padding(appDp(10))
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(appDp(8)))
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.70f))
                            .border(appDp(1), Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f), RoundedCornerShape(appDp(8)))
                            .padding(horizontal = appDp(10), vertical = appDp(5)),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(6)),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            aBadge,
                            fontSize = appSp(11),
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = Palette.Glow,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            if (channel != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.height(appDp(10)))
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(12), Alignment.CenterHorizontally),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    PlayerControlButton(
                        icon = if (isPlaying) Ic.Pause else Ic.Play,
                        description = if (isPlaying) Ar.pause else Ar.play,
                        onClick = {
                            previewExo?.let { exo ->
                                if (exo.isPlaying) exo.pause() else exo.play()
                            }
                        },
                    )
                    PlayerControlButton(
                        icon = if (muted) Ic.VolumeOff else Ic.VolumeUp,
                        description = if (muted) Ar.unmute else Ar.mute,
                        onClick = {
                            muted = !muted
                            previewExo?.volume = if (muted) 0f else 1f
                        },
                    )
                    PlayerControlButton(
                        icon = if (isFavorite) Ic.Heart else Ic.HeartOutline,
                        description = Ar.favorites,
                        onClick = onToggleFavorite,
                        isRed = true,
                    )
                    PlayerControlButton(icon = Ic.Search, description = Ar.guide, onClick = { onGuide(channel) })
                    PlayerControlButton(icon = Ic.Aspect, description = Ar.fullscreen, onClick = openFullScreen)
                }
            }
        }

        // Card 2: EPG Schedule (shown on Tablet/TV where vertical space allows both video and schedule)
        if (showEpg) {
            GlassPanel(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .weight(1f),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(appDp(24)),
                padding = appDp(16),
            ) {
                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    DotTitle(Ar.epgTitle, Palette.Gold, fontSize = appSp(14))
                    Text(today, fontSize = appSp(11), color = Palette.TextDim)
                }

                Spacer(Modifier.height(appDp(10)))

                if (channel == null) {
                    Text(Ar.pickChannel, fontSize = appSp(13), color = Palette.TextDim)
                } else {
                    val now = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000
                    val past = programs.lastOrNull { it.end <= now }
                    val current = programs.firstOrNull { now in it.start until it.end }
                    val upcoming = programs.filter { it.start > (current?.start ?: now) }.take(2)

                    if (past == null && current == null && upcoming.isEmpty()) {
                        Text(Ar.noEpgShort, fontSize = appSp(13), color = Palette.TextDim)
                    } else {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(8))) {
                            past?.let { prog ->
                                EpgScheduleRow(
                                    time = "${formatClock(prog.start)} - ${formatClock(prog.end)}",
                                    title = prog.title.ifBlank { Ar.noName },
                                    state = EpgRowState.ENDED,
                                    onClick = { onGuide(channel) },
                                )
                            }
                            current?.let { prog ->
                                EpgScheduleRow(
                                    time = "${formatClock(prog.start)} - ${formatClock(prog.end)}",
                                    title = prog.title.ifBlank { Ar.noName },
                                    state = EpgRowState.NOW,
                                    onClick = { onGuide(channel) },
                                )
                            }
                            upcoming.forEach { prog ->
                                EpgScheduleRow(
                                    time = "${formatClock(prog.start)} - ${formatClock(prog.end)}",
                                    title = prog.title.ifBlank { Ar.noName },
                                    state = EpgRowState.SOON,
                                    onClick = { onGuide(channel) },
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

internal enum class EpgRowState { ENDED, NOW, SOON }

@Composable
internal fun EpgScheduleRow(
    time: String,
    title: String,
    state: EpgRowState,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(appDp(12))
    val isNow = state == EpgRowState.NOW
    val bg: Brush = if (isNow) Palette.ActiveFill else Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(Palette.Glass, Palette.Glass))
    val borderColor = if (isNow) Palette.Glow else Palette.GlassBorder

    Row(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(shape)
            .background(bg)
            .border(appDp(1), borderColor, shape)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = appDp(12), vertical = appDp(9)),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier.weight(1f),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(10)),
        ) {
            Text(
                time,
                fontSize = appSp(11),
                fontWeight = if (isNow) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                color = if (isNow) Palette.Gold else Palette.TextDim,
                maxLines = 1,
            )
            Text(
                title,
                fontSize = appSp(13),
                fontWeight = if (isNow) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                color = if (state == EpgRowState.ENDED) Palette.TextDim else Palette.Text,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            )
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(appDp(8)))
        when (state) {
            EpgRowState.ENDED -> Tag(Ar.ended, Palette.TextDim, fontSize = appSp(10))
            EpgRowState.NOW -> Tag(Ar.onAirNow, Palette.Live, filled = true, fontSize = appSp(10))
            EpgRowState.SOON -> Tag(Ar.soon, Palette.Glow, fontSize = appSp(10))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun PlayerControlButton(
    icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector,
    description: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    isRed: Boolean = false,
) {
    var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val bg = when {
        isRed -> Palette.Live.copy(alpha = if (focused) 0.55f else 0.30f)
        focused -> Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.55f)
        else -> Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f)
    }
    val borderColor = when {
        focused -> Palette.Glow
        isRed -> Palette.Live.copy(alpha = 0.55f)
        else -> Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
    }
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(appDp(40))
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(bg)
            .border(if (focused) appDp(2) else appDp(1), borderColor, CircleShape)
            .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused }
            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        Icon(
            icon,
            contentDescription = description,
            tint = if (isRed) Palette.Live else Color.White,
            modifier = Modifier.size(appDp(18)),
        )
    }
}

internal val IdleChannelRowFill: Brush get() = SolidColor(Palette.Glass)
internal val PreviewShadeBrush: Brush get() = Brush.verticalGradient(
    listOf(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.45f), Color.Transparent, Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.65f)),
)
internal val PreviewPlaceholderBrush: Brush get() = Brush.radialGradient(
    listOf(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.32f), Palette.DeepBlue, Color.Black),
)

internal const val FAV_CAT_ID = "__FAVORITES__"
internal const val ALL_CAT_ID = "__ALL__"

@Composable
fun LiveScreen(onBack: () -> Unit, onPlay: (PlayRequest) -> Unit) {
    val vm = appViewModel { LiveViewModel(it) }
    val state by vm.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    val activeAccountId by LocalContainer.current.prefs.activeAccountId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(
        initialValue = LocalContainer.current.prefs.currentActiveAccountId(),
    )
    val favorites by LocalContainer.current.prefs.favorites.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())
    val favoriteIds = remember(favorites, activeAccountId) {
        favorites.forAccount(activeAccountId).filter { it.kind == Kind.LIVE }.map { it.id }.toSet()
    }
    var guideFor by remember { mutableStateOf<Channel?>(null) }
    var previewChannel by remember { mutableStateOf<Channel?>(null) }
    var showManageLists by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var editingList by remember { mutableStateOf<ChannelListEntity?>(null) }
    var editingListIds by remember { mutableStateOf<List<String>>(emptyList()) }
    var allChannelsForPicker by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }
    var managingChannel by remember { mutableStateOf<Channel?>(null) }

    LaunchedEffect(state.categories.isNotEmpty()) {
        if (state.categories.isNotEmpty()) {
            val savedCat = focusNav.getSelectedCategory(Routes.LIVE)
            if (!savedCat.isNullOrBlank() && savedCat != state.selectedId) {
                vm.select(savedCat)
            }
            val savedQ = focusNav.getSearchQuery(Routes.LIVE)
            if (savedQ.isNotEmpty() && savedQ != state.query) {
                vm.onQuery(savedQ)
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(state.selectedId) {
        focusNav.saveSelectedCategory(Routes.LIVE, state.selectedId)
    }

    LaunchedEffect(state.query) {
        focusNav.saveSearchQuery(Routes.LIVE, state.query)
    }

    val rawChannels = state.visibleChannels
    val channels = remember(rawChannels, state.selectedId, favoriteIds, state.searchResults) {
        if (state.selectedId == FAV_CAT_ID && state.searchResults == null) {
            rawChannels.filter { it.id in favoriteIds }
        } else {
            rawChannels
        }
    }

    val playScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var openingPlayer by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val channelIdSet = remember(channels) {
        HashSet<String>(channels.size).apply { channels.forEach { add(it.id) } }
    }

    val playAt: (Int) -> Unit = { index ->
        if (!openingPlayer && channels.isNotEmpty() && index in channels.indices) {
            openingPlayer = true
            previewChannel = null
            SahabPlayer.releasePreviewPlayers()
            val snapshot = channels
            playScope.launch {
                try {
                    val items = if (snapshot.size > 300) {
                        withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
                            snapshot.map { PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, it.id, it.name, icon = it.icon, url = it.url) }
                        }
                    } else {
                        snapshot.map { PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, it.id, it.name, icon = it.icon, url = it.url) }
                    }
                    onPlay(PlayRequest(items, index))
                } finally {
                    openingPlayer = false
                }
            }
        }
    }
    val playChannel: (Channel) -> Unit = { ch ->
        val idx = channels.indexOfFirst { it.id == ch.id }
        if (idx >= 0) {
            playAt(idx)
        } else {
            previewChannel = null
            SahabPlayer.releasePreviewPlayers()
            onPlay(PlayRequest(listOf(PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, ch.id, ch.name, icon = ch.icon, url = ch.url)), 0))
        }
    }

    val activePreview = previewChannel?.takeIf { it.id in channelIdSet }

    val handleChannelActivate: (Int) -> Unit = { idx ->
        val ch = channels.getOrNull(idx)
        if (ch != null && activePreview?.id != ch.id) {
            previewChannel = ch
        } else {
            playAt(idx)
        }
    }

    Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        val displayMode = LocalDisplayMode.current
        when (displayMode) {
            DisplayMode.TABLET_OR_TV -> {
                WideLiveLayout(
                    state = state,
                    channels = channels,
                    favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
                    previewChannel = activePreview,
                    onPreview = { previewChannel = it },
                    onPlayAt = handleChannelActivate,
                    onPlayChannel = playChannel,
                    onToggleFavorite = vm::toggleFavorite,
                    onManageChannel = { managingChannel = it },
                    onManageLists = { showManageLists = true },
                    onGuide = { guideFor = it },
                    onSelect = vm::select,
                    onQuery = vm::onQuery,
                    onBack = onBack,
                    onRetry = { state.selectedId?.let(vm::select) },
                )
            }
            DisplayMode.PHONE_LANDSCAPE -> {
                PhoneLandscapeLiveLayout(
                    state = state,
                    channels = channels,
                    favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
                    previewChannel = activePreview,
                    onPreview = { previewChannel = it },
                    onPlayAt = handleChannelActivate,
                    onPlayChannel = playChannel,
                    onToggleFavorite = vm::toggleFavorite,
                    onManageChannel = { managingChannel = it },
                    onManageLists = { showManageLists = true },
                    onGuide = { guideFor = it },
                    onSelect = vm::select,
                    onQuery = vm::onQuery,
                    onBack = onBack,
                    onRetry = { state.selectedId?.let(vm::select) },
                )
            }
            DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT -> {
                PhonePortraitLiveLayout(
                    state = state,
                    channels = channels,
                    favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
                    previewChannel = activePreview,
                    onPreview = { previewChannel = it },
                    onPlayAt = handleChannelActivate,
                    onPlayChannel = playChannel,
                    onToggleFavorite = vm::toggleFavorite,
                    onManageChannel = { managingChannel = it },
                    onManageLists = { showManageLists = true },
                    onGuide = { guideFor = it },
                    onSelect = vm::select,
                    onQuery = vm::onQuery,
                    onBack = onBack,
                    onRetry = { state.selectedId?.let(vm::select) },
                )
            }
        }
    }

    guideFor?.let { channel ->
        EpgDialog(
            channelName = channel.name,
            channelId = channel.id,
            onDismiss = {
                guideFor = null
                focusNav.onScreenResumed(Routes.LIVE)
            }
        )
    }

    if (showManageLists) {
        CustomListsManagerDialog(
            lists = state.customLists,
            onCreateList = vm::createCustomList,
            onEditChannels = { list ->
                showManageLists = false
                editingList = list
                playScope.launch {
                    allChannelsForPicker = vm.allLiveChannels()
                    editingListIds = vm.listChannelIds(list.listId)
                }
            },
            onDeleteList = vm::deleteCustomList,
            onDismiss = {
                showManageLists = false
                focusNav.onScreenResumed(Routes.LIVE)
            },
        )
    }

    editingList?.let { list ->
        CustomListChannelPickerDialog(
            list = list,
            allChannels = allChannelsForPicker,
            initialOrderedIds = editingListIds,
            onSave = { orderedIds ->
                vm.saveListChannels(list.listId, orderedIds)
                editingList = null
                focusNav.onScreenResumed(Routes.LIVE)
            },
            onDismiss = {
                editingList = null
                focusNav.onScreenResumed(Routes.LIVE)
            },
        )
    }

    managingChannel?.let { ch ->
        ChannelManageContextSheet(
            channel = ch,
            isFavorite = ch.id in favoriteIds,
            customLists = state.customLists,
            onToggleFavorite = { vm.toggleFavorite(ch) },
            onToggleInList = { listId -> vm.toggleChannelInList(listId, ch.id) },
            onMoveUp = { vm.moveChannelOrder(ch.id, -1) },
            onMoveDown = { vm.moveChannelOrder(ch.id, 1) },
            onHideChannel = { vm.hideChannel(ch.id) },
            onDismiss = {
                managingChannel = null
                focusNav.onScreenResumed(Routes.LIVE)
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun WideLiveLayout(
    state: LiveUiState,
    channels: List<Channel>,
    favoriteIds: Set<String>,
    previewChannel: Channel?,
    onPreview: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onPlayAt: (Int) -> Unit,
    onPlayChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onToggleFavorite: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageLists: () -> Unit,
    onGuide: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onQuery: (String) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onRetry: () -> Unit,
) {
    val themeCustomization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Rtl) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            SahabTopBar(current = Routes.LIVE, onBack = onBack)

            Row(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .weight(1f)
                    .padding(horizontal = appDp(24), vertical = appDp(10)),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(22)),
            ) {
                // Column 1 (Right in RTL): Search + Categories sidebar
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.24f)
                        .fillMaxHeight(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(10)),
                ) {
                    SearchBox(
                        state.query,
                        onQuery,
                        Ar.searchChannels,
                        Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )
                    CategorySidebar(
                        categories = state.categories,
                        customLists = state.customLists,
                        selectedId = state.selectedId,
                        onSelect = onSelect,
                        onManageLists = onManageLists,
                        totalCount = state.totalCount,
                        favCount = favoriteIds.size,
                        categoryCounts = state.categoryCounts,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .weight(1f),
                    )
                }

                // Column 2 (Center): Live Channel List — wider so full channel names fit
                ChannelColumn(
                    state = state,
                    channels = channels,
                    favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
                    previewId = previewChannel?.id,
                    onPreview = onPreview,
                    onPlayAt = onPlayAt,
                    onToggleFavorite = onToggleFavorite,
                    onManageChannel = onManageChannel,
                    onGuide = onGuide,
                    onRetry = onRetry,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(0.39f)
                        .fillMaxHeight(),
                )

                // Column 3 (Left in RTL): Video Preview & EPG Schedule
                if (themeCustomization.isVisible("live_preview")) PreviewPanel(
                    channel = previewChannel,
                    isFavorite = previewChannel?.id in favoriteIds,
                    onPlay = { previewChannel?.let(onPlayChannel) },
                    onToggleFavorite = { previewChannel?.let(onToggleFavorite) },
                    onGuide = onGuide,
                    showEpg = themeCustomization.isVisible("live_epg"),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(0.37f)
                        .fillMaxHeight(),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun PhoneLandscapeLiveLayout(
    state: LiveUiState,
    channels: List<Channel>,
    favoriteIds: Set<String>,
    previewChannel: Channel?,
    onPreview: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onPlayAt: (Int) -> Unit,
    onPlayChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onToggleFavorite: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageLists: () -> Unit,
    onGuide: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onQuery: (String) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onRetry: () -> Unit,
) {
    val themeCustomization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Rtl) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            SahabTopBar(current = Routes.LIVE, onBack = onBack)

            LazyRow(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 14.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            ) {
                item(key = FAV_CAT_ID) {
                    Chip(Ar.favorites, state.selectedId == FAV_CAT_ID, { onSelect(FAV_CAT_ID) })
                }
                item(key = ALL_CAT_ID) {
                    Chip(Ar.all, state.selectedId == ALL_CAT_ID, { onSelect(ALL_CAT_ID) })
                }
                item(key = "manage_custom_lists") {
                    Chip("إدارة القوائم", false, onManageLists)
                }
                items(state.customLists, key = { "custom_${it.listId}" }) { list ->
                    val key = "$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX${list.listId}"
                    Chip(list.name, state.selectedId == key, { onSelect(key) })
                }
                items(state.categories, key = { it.id }) { cat ->
                    Chip(cat.name, cat.id == state.selectedId, { onSelect(cat.id) })
                }
            }

            Row(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .weight(1f)
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            ) {
                // Right pane in RTL: Search + Channel list
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.54f)
                        .fillMaxHeight(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                ) {
                    SearchBox(
                        state.query,
                        onQuery,
                        Ar.searchChannels,
                        Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )
                    ChannelColumn(
                        state = state,
                        channels = channels,
                        favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
                        previewId = previewChannel?.id,
                        onPreview = onPreview,
                        onPlayAt = onPlayAt,
                        onToggleFavorite = onToggleFavorite,
                        onManageChannel = onManageChannel,
                        onGuide = onGuide,
                        onRetry = onRetry,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .weight(1f),
                    )
                }

                // Left pane in RTL: Compact Live Video Preview without EPG card overflow
                if (themeCustomization.isVisible("live_preview")) PreviewPanel(
                    channel = previewChannel,
                    isFavorite = previewChannel?.id in favoriteIds,
                    onPlay = { previewChannel?.let(onPlayChannel) },
                    onToggleFavorite = { previewChannel?.let(onToggleFavorite) },
                    onGuide = onGuide,
                    showEpg = false,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(0.46f)
                        .fillMaxHeight(),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun PhonePortraitLiveLayout(
    state: LiveUiState,
    channels: List<Channel>,
    favoriteIds: Set<String>,
    previewChannel: Channel?,
    onPreview: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onPlayAt: (Int) -> Unit,
    onPlayChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onToggleFavorite: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageChannel: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onManageLists: () -> Unit,
    onGuide: (Channel) -> Unit,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onQuery: (String) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onRetry: () -> Unit,
) {
    val themeCustomization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        SahabTopBar(current = Routes.LIVE, onBack = onBack)

        if (previewChannel != null && themeCustomization.isVisible("live_preview")) {
            PreviewPanel(
                channel = previewChannel,
                isFavorite = previewChannel.id in favoriteIds,
                onPlay = { onPlayChannel(previewChannel) },
                onToggleFavorite = { onToggleFavorite(previewChannel) },
                onGuide = onGuide,
                showEpg = false,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
            )
        }

        SearchBox(
            state.query,
            onQuery,
            Ar.searchChannels,
            Modifier.padding(horizontal = appDp(12), vertical = appDp(4)),
        )
        LazyRow(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = appDp(4)),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = appDp(12)),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(appDp(8)),
        ) {
            item(key = FAV_CAT_ID) {
                Chip(Ar.favorites, state.selectedId == FAV_CAT_ID, { onSelect(FAV_CAT_ID) })
            }
            item(key = ALL_CAT_ID) {
                Chip(Ar.all, state.selectedId == ALL_CAT_ID, { onSelect(ALL_CAT_ID) })
            }
            item(key = "manage_custom_lists") {
                Chip("إدارة القوائم", false, onManageLists)
            }
            items(state.customLists, key = { "custom_${it.listId}" }) { list ->
                val key = "$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX${list.listId}"
                Chip(list.name, state.selectedId == key, { onSelect(key) })
            }
            items(state.categories, key = { it.id }) { cat ->
                Chip(cat.name, cat.id == state.selectedId, { onSelect(cat.id) })
            }
        }
        ChannelColumn(
            state = state,
            channels = channels,
            favoriteIds = favoriteIds,
            previewId = previewChannel?.id,
            onPreview = onPreview,
            onPlayAt = onPlayAt,
            onToggleFavorite = onToggleFavorite,
            onManageChannel = onManageChannel,
            onGuide = onGuide,
            onRetry = onRetry,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = appDp(12)),
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun appSp(n: Int): TextUnit = (n * LocalAppScale.current).sp

@Composable
internal fun appDp(n: Int): Dp = (n * LocalAppScale.current).dp

@Composable
internal fun appDp(n: Float): Dp = (n * LocalAppScale.current).dp

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/live/LiveViewModel.kt ===

internal const val CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX = "custom_list:"

@Immutable
data class LiveUiState(
    val categories: List<Category> = emptyList(),
    val customLists: List<ChannelListEntity> = emptyList(),
    val hiddenChannelIds: Set<String> = emptySet(),
    val selectedId: String? = ALL_CAT_ID,
    val channels: List<Channel> = emptyList(),
    val visibleChannels: List<Channel> = emptyList(),
    val categoryCounts: Map<String, Int> = emptyMap(),
    val totalCount: Int = 0,
    val query: String = "",
    val searchResults: List<Channel>? = null,
    val loadingCategories: Boolean = true,
    val loadingChannels: Boolean = false,
    val searching: Boolean = false,
    val error: String? = null,
) {
    val busy: Boolean get() = loadingChannels || searching
}

class LiveViewModel(private val container: AppContainer) : ViewModel() {

    private val repo = container.repo
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(LiveUiState())
    val state = _state.asStateFlow()
    private val visibleMutex = Mutex()

    private var channelsJob: Job? = null
    private var searchJob: Job? = null
    private var searchPool: List<Channel>? = null
    private var indexedPool: List<Pair<Channel, String>>? = null

    private suspend fun updateWithVisible(transform: (LiveUiState) -> LiveUiState) {
        visibleMutex.withLock {
            val candidate = transform(_state.value)
            val source = candidate.searchResults ?: candidate.channels
            val hidden = candidate.hiddenChannelIds
            val visible = when {
                hidden.isEmpty() -> source
                source.size <= 200 -> source.filterNot { it.id in hidden }
                else -> withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { source.filterNot { it.id in hidden } }
            }
            _state.update { latest ->
                transform(latest).copy(visibleChannels = visible)
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun updateSearchPool(channels: List<Channel>) {
        searchPool = channels
        indexedPool = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            channels.map { it to it.name.normalizeForSearch() }
        }
    }

    private val dao = container.channelDb.channelDao()

    init {
        loadCategories()
        observeCustomTvState()
    }

    private fun observeCustomTvState() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            container.prefs.activeAccountId.collectLatest { accId ->
                dao.observeLists(accId).collect { lists ->
                    _state.update { it.copy(customLists = lists) }
                }
            }
        }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dao.observeHiddenIds().collect { hidden ->
                val hiddenSet = hidden.toSet()
                updateWithVisible { it.copy(hiddenChannelIds = hiddenSet) }
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun computeCounts(allChannels: List<Channel>): Map<String, Int> =
        withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val counts = HashMap<String, Int>()
            allChannels.forEach { ch ->
                val cid = ch.categoryId
                if (cid.isNotBlank()) {
                    counts[cid] = (counts[cid] ?: 0) + 1
                }
            }
            counts
        }

    fun loadCategories() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _state.update { it.copy(loadingCategories = true, error = null) }
            try {
                val categories = repo.liveCategories()
                val cachedAll = repo.peekAllLiveChannels()
                if (!cachedAll.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                    updateSearchPool(cachedAll)
                    val counts = computeCounts(cachedAll)
                    updateWithVisible {
                        it.copy(
                            categories = categories,
                            selectedId = ALL_CAT_ID,
                            channels = cachedAll,
                            loadingCategories = false,
                            loadingChannels = false,
                            totalCount = cachedAll.size,
                            categoryCounts = counts,
                        )
                    }
                    return@launch
                }

                // Cold start: display categories immediately and load the first category right away
                val firstCatId = categories.firstOrNull()?.id ?: ALL_CAT_ID
                _state.update {
                    it.copy(
                        categories = categories,
                        selectedId = firstCatId,
                        loadingCategories = false,
                    )
                }
                select(firstCatId)

                // Fetch full channel pool & category counts in background without blocking UI
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    val allChannels = runCatching { repo.liveChannels(null) }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
                    if (allChannels.isNotEmpty()) {
                        updateSearchPool(allChannels)
                        val counts = computeCounts(allChannels)
                        updateWithVisible { current ->
                            current.copy(
                                totalCount = allChannels.size,
                                categoryCounts = counts + current.categoryCounts,
                                channels = if (current.selectedId == ALL_CAT_ID || current.channels.isEmpty()) allChannels else current.channels,
                                loadingChannels = if (current.selectedId == ALL_CAT_ID) false else current.loadingChannels,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _state.update { it.copy(loadingCategories = false, error = e.userMessage()) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun select(categoryId: String) {
        channelsJob?.cancel()
        if (categoryId.startsWith(CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX)) {
            val listId = categoryId.removePrefix(CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX)
            _state.update { it.copy(selectedId = categoryId, loadingChannels = true, error = null) }
            channelsJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                val orderedIds = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { dao.itemsOfList(listId) }
                val pool = allLiveChannels()
                val byId = pool.associateBy { it.id }
                val listChannels = orderedIds.mapNotNull { byId[it] }
                updateWithVisible {
                    it.copy(
                        selectedId = categoryId,
                        channels = listChannels,
                        loadingChannels = false,
                        error = null,
                    )
                }
            }
            return
        }
        if (categoryId == FAV_CAT_ID) {
            val pool = searchPool
            viewModelScope.launch {
                updateWithVisible {
                    it.copy(
                        selectedId = FAV_CAT_ID,
                        channels = pool ?: it.channels,
                        loadingChannels = false,
                        error = null,
                    )
                }
            }
            return
        }
        if (categoryId == ALL_CAT_ID) {
            val cached = searchPool
            if (!cached.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    updateWithVisible {
                        it.copy(
                            selectedId = ALL_CAT_ID,
                            channels = cached,
                            loadingChannels = false,
                            error = null,
                        )
                    }
                }
                return
            }
        }
        _state.update { it.copy(selectedId = categoryId, loadingChannels = true, error = null) }
        channelsJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val targetId = if (categoryId == ALL_CAT_ID) null else categoryId
                val channels = repo.liveChannels(targetId)
                if (categoryId == ALL_CAT_ID) updateSearchPool(channels)
                updateWithVisible {
                    it.copy(
                        channels = channels,
                        loadingChannels = false,
                        categoryCounts = if (categoryId != ALL_CAT_ID && categoryId != FAV_CAT_ID) {
                            it.categoryCounts + (categoryId to channels.size)
                        } else it.categoryCounts,
                    )
                }
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                updateWithVisible {
                    it.copy(loadingChannels = false, channels = emptyList(), error = e.userMessage())
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun onQuery(query: String) {
        _state.update { it.copy(query = query) }
        searchJob?.cancel()
        val needle = query.normalizeForSearch().trim()
        if (needle.length < 2) {
            viewModelScope.launch {
                updateWithVisible { it.copy(searchResults = null, searching = false) }
            }
            return
        }
        searchJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            delay(300)
            _state.update { it.copy(searching = true) }
            try {
                val indexed = indexedPool ?: run {
                    val pool = searchPool ?: repo.liveChannels(null).also { updateSearchPool(it) }
                    indexedPool ?: withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
                        pool.map { it to it.name.normalizeForSearch() }
                    }
                }
                val results = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
                    indexed.asSequence()
                        .filter { it.second.contains(needle) }
                        .map { it.first }
                        .take(300)
                        .toList()
                }
                updateWithVisible { it.copy(searchResults = results, searching = false) }
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                updateWithVisible { it.copy(searching = false, searchResults = emptyList(), error = e.userMessage()) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun toggleFavorite(channel: Channel) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            container.prefs.toggleFavorite(
                FavItem(Kind.LIVE, channel.id, channel.name, channel.icon, url = channel.url, accountId = container.prefs.currentActiveAccountId())
            )
        }
    }

    suspend fun allLiveChannels(): List<Channel> {
        searchPool?.let { return it }
        val loaded = runCatching { repo.liveChannels(null) }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        if (loaded.isNotEmpty()) updateSearchPool(loaded)
        return loaded
    }

    suspend fun listChannelIds(listId: String): List<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        dao.itemsOfList(listId)
    }

    fun createCustomList(name: String) {
        val clean = name.trim()
        if (clean.isEmpty()) return
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val accId = container.prefs.currentActiveAccountId()
            dao.upsertList(
                ChannelListEntity(
                    listId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                    name = clean,
                    createdAt = now,
                    updatedAt = now,
                    accountId = accId,
                )
            )
        }
    }

    fun deleteCustomList(listId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            dao.deleteListAndItems(listId)
            if (_state.value.selectedId == "$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX$listId") {
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { select(ALL_CAT_ID) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveListChannels(listId: String, orderedChannelIds: List<String>) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            dao.replaceListItems(listId, orderedChannelIds)
            if (_state.value.selectedId == "$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX$listId") {
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { select("$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX$listId") }
            }
        }
    }

    fun toggleChannelInList(listId: String, channelId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            dao.toggleChannelInList(listId, channelId)
            if (_state.value.selectedId == "$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX$listId") {
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { select("$CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX$listId") }
            }
        }
    }

    fun hideChannel(channelId: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            dao.setHidden(channelId, true, System.currentTimeMillis())
        }
    }

    fun moveChannelOrder(channelId: String, delta: Int) {
        val current = _state.value.channels.toMutableList()
        val idx = current.indexOfFirst { it.id == channelId }
        val target = (idx + delta).coerceIn(0, (current.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        if (idx < 0 || idx == target) return
        val moved = current.removeAt(idx)
        current.add(target, moved)
        viewModelScope.launch {
            updateWithVisible { it.copy(channels = current) }
        }

        val selected = _state.value.selectedId
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            if (selected != null && selected.startsWith(CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX)) {
                val listId = selected.removePrefix(CUSTOM_LIST_PREFIX)
                dao.replaceListItems(listId, current.map { it.id })
            } else {
                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                dao.setSortOrder(current[idx].id, idx, now)
                dao.setSortOrder(current[target].id, target, now)
            }
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/live/custom/ChannelManageContextSheet.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.live.custom

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape

import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.compose.ui.window.Dialog
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.Channel
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.channel.ChannelListEntity
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.DotTitle
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.GlassButton
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.GlassPanel
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette

@Composable
internal fun ChannelManageContextSheet(
    channel: Channel,
    isFavorite: Boolean,
    customLists: List<ChannelListEntity>,
    onToggleFavorite: () -> Unit,
    onToggleInList: (String) -> Unit,
    onMoveUp: () -> Unit,
    onMoveDown: () -> Unit,
    onHideChannel: () -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 20.dp,
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                Text(
                    text = channel.name,
                    fontSize = 17.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = Palette.Text,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )

                GlassButton(
                    text = if (isFavorite) "إزالة من المفضلة" else "إضافة إلى المفضلة",
                    primary = true,
                    onClick = {
                        onToggleFavorite()
                        onDismiss()
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )

                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "تحريك للأعلى",
                        onClick = {
                            onMoveUp()
                            onDismiss()
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "تحريك للأسفل",
                        onClick = {
                            onMoveDown()
                            onDismiss()
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }

                if (customLists.isNotEmpty()) {
                    DotTitle("إضافة / إزالة من قائمة مخصصة", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    customLists.forEach { list ->
                        GlassButton(
                            text = list.name,
                            onClick = {
                                onToggleInList(list.listId)
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        )
                    }
                }

                GlassButton(
                    text = "إخفاء القناة",
                    onClick = {
                        onHideChannel()
                        onDismiss()
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )

                GlassButton(
                    text = "إلغاء",
                    onClick = onDismiss,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@Composable
internal fun CustomListChannelPickerDialog(
    list: ChannelListEntity,
    allChannels: List<Channel>,
    initialOrderedIds: List<String>,
    onSave: (List<String>) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    var query by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var orderedIds by remember(list.listId, initialOrderedIds) { mutableStateOf(initialOrderedIds) }
    var filteredChannels by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }

    LaunchedEffect(allChannels, query, orderedIds) {
        if (query.isNotBlank()) delay(150L)
        val selectedSet = orderedIds.toHashSet()
        val result = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val needle = query.normalizeForSearch().trim()
            if (needle.isBlank()) {
                val byId = allChannels.associateBy { it.id }
                val selected = orderedIds.mapNotNull { byId[it] }
                val rest = allChannels.asSequence().filter { it.id !in selectedSet }.take(150).toList()
                selected + rest
            } else {
                allChannels.asSequence()
                    .filter { it.name.normalizeForSearch().contains(needle) }
                    .take(150)
                    .toList()
            }
        }
        filteredChannels = result
    }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(max = 540.dp),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 18.dp,
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = "قنوات قائمة: ${list.name}",
                            fontSize = 16.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                            color = Color.White,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "${orderedIds.size} قناة مختارة",
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            color = Palette.FocusCyan,
                        )
                    }
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "حفظ",
                        primary = true,
                        onClick = { onSave(orderedIds) },
                    )
                }

                SearchBox(
                    value = query,
                    onValueChange = { query = it },
                    placeholder = "ابحث عن قناة لإضافتها أو إزالتها...",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )

                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 4.dp),
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(filteredChannels, key = { idx, ch -> "${ch.id}_$idx" }) { _, ch ->
                        val checked = ch.id in orderedIds
                        GlassCard(
                            onClick = {
                                orderedIds = if (checked) orderedIds - ch.id else orderedIds + ch.id
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                            selected = checked,
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                            ) {
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = checked,
                                    onCheckedChange = null,
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(
                                        checkedColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                                        uncheckedColor = Palette.TextDim,
                                        checkmarkColor = Palette.CinematicBg,
                                    ),
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = ch.name,
                                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (checked) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.live.custom

import androidx.compose.foundation.background

                                    text = "حذف",
                                    onClick = { onDeleteList(list.listId) },
                                )

                        }

                }

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/login/CloudLoginScreen.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.login

import android.app.Activity
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardOptions
import androidx.compose.foundation.verticalScroll
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.focus.FocusRequester
import androidx.compose.ui.focus.focusRequester
import androidx.compose.ui.focus.focusProperties
import androidx.compose.ui.focus.onFocusChanged
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.ImeAction
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import androidx.credentials.CredentialManager
import androidx.credentials.GetCredentialRequest
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GetGoogleIdOption
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GoogleIdTokenCredential
import com.saydlawy.sahab.AppContainer
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalContainer
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.appViewModel
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.*
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class CloudLoginState(
    val email: String = "",
    val pass: String = "",
    val loading: Boolean = false,
    val error: String? = null,
    val isRegisterMode: Boolean = false
)

class CloudLoginViewModel(private val container: AppContainer) : ViewModel() {
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(CloudLoginState())
    val state = _state.asStateFlow()

    fun onEmail(value: String) = _state.update { it.copy(email = value, error = null) }
    fun onPass(value: String) = _state.update { it.copy(pass = value, error = null) }
    fun toggleMode() = _state.update { it.copy(isRegisterMode = !it.isRegisterMode, error = null) }
    fun onError(message: String) = _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, error = message) }

    fun signInWithGoogle(idToken: String, onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        _state.update { it.copy(loading = true, error = null) }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                container.authManager.signInWithGoogle(idToken)
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false) }
                onSuccess()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, error = e.message ?: "فشل تسجيل الدخول بجوجل") }
            }
        }
    }

    fun submit(onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        val current = _state.value
        if (current.email.isBlank() || current.pass.isBlank()) {
            _state.update { it.copy(error = "يرجى إدخال البريد وكلمة المرور") }
            return
        }
        _state.update { it.copy(loading = true, error = null) }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (current.isRegisterMode) {
                    container.authManager.signUpWithEmail(current.email, current.pass)
                } else {
                    container.authManager.signInWithEmail(current.email, current.pass)
                }
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false) }
                onSuccess()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, error = e.message ?: "حدث خطأ") }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun CloudLoginScreen(
    onSuccess: () -> Unit,
    onSkip: () -> Unit,
    skipLabel: String = "تخطي الآن (بدون حفظ سحابي)",
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val vm = appViewModel { CloudLoginViewModel(it) }
    val state by vm.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val serverClientId = stringResource(id = com.saydlawy.sahab.R.string.default_web_client_id)

    val emailFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val passFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val loginFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val toggleModeFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val googleFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val skipFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        withFrameNanos { }
        runCatching { emailFocus.requestFocus() }
    }

    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val googleSignIn: () -> Unit = {
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val credentialManager = CredentialManager.create(context)
                val googleIdOption = GetGoogleIdOption.Builder()
                    .setServerClientId(serverClientId)
                    .setFilterByAuthorizedAccounts(false)
                    .build()
                val request = GetCredentialRequest.Builder()
                    .addCredentialOption(googleIdOption)
                    .build()
                val result = credentialManager.getCredential(context, request)
                val credential = result.credential
                if (credential is androidx.credentials.CustomCredential &&
                    credential.type == GoogleIdTokenCredential.TYPE_GOOGLE_ID_TOKEN_CREDENTIAL
                ) {
                    val googleIdTokenCredential = GoogleIdTokenCredential.createFrom(credential.data)
                    val token = googleIdTokenCredential.idToken
                    vm.signInWithGoogle(token, onSuccess)
                } else {
                    vm.onError("تعذّر التحقق من بيانات اعتماد Google")
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                if (e !is androidx.credentials.exceptions.GetCredentialCancellationException) {
                    vm.onError("فشل تسجيل الدخول عبر Google: ${e.localizedMessage ?: e.javaClass.simpleName}")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // الخلفية شفافة ليعمل DreamyBackground من AppRoot
    Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .widthIn(max = 480.dp)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .imePadding() // رفع الشاشة مع لوحة المفاتيح
                .padding(24.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            GlassPanel(
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(32.dp),
                strong = true,
                padding = 28.dp
            ) {
                Column(
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    BrandLogo(size = 80.dp, bright = true)
                    Text("حساب سحاب", fontSize = 28.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text(
                        "سجل دخولك لحفظ اشتراكك ومفضلتك سحابياً",
                        fontSize = 14.sp, color = Palette.TextDim, textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))

                    GlassTextField(
                        value = state.email,
                        onValueChange = vm::onEmail,
                        placeholder = "البريد الإلكتروني",
                        keyboardType = KeyboardType.Email,
                        imeAction = ImeAction.Next,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        inputModifier = Modifier.focusRequester(emailFocus).focusProperties { down = passFocus },
                    )

                    GlassTextField(
                        value = state.pass,
                        onValueChange = vm::onPass,
                        placeholder = "كلمة المرور",
                        keyboardType = KeyboardType.Password,
                        password = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        inputModifier = Modifier.focusRequester(passFocus).focusProperties { down = loginFocus; up = emailFocus },
                    )

                    state.error?.let {
                        Text(it, color = Palette.Danger, fontSize = 13.sp, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                    }

                    GlassButton(
                        text = if (state.loading) "جاري الاتصال..." else if (state.isRegisterMode) "إنشاء حساب" else "تسجيل الدخول",
                        onClick = { if (!state.loading) vm.submit(onSuccess) },
                        primary = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().focusRequester(loginFocus).focusProperties { down = toggleModeFocus; up = passFocus }
                    )

                    var toggleFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    TextButton(
                        onClick = vm::toggleMode,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .focusRequester(toggleModeFocus)
                            .focusProperties { up = loginFocus; down = googleFocus }
                            .onFocusChanged { toggleFocused = it.isFocused }
                            .then(
                                if (toggleFocused) Modifier.border(1.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan, RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                else Modifier,
                            ),
                    ) {
                        Text(if (state.isRegisterMode) "لديك حساب؟ سجل دخولك" else "ليس لديك حساب؟ أنشئ حساباً", color = Palette.Glow)
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

            GlassButton(
                text = "تسجيل الدخول باستخدام Google",
                onClick = googleSignIn,
                primary = false,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().focusRequester(googleFocus).focusProperties { down = skipFocus; up = toggleModeFocus }
            )

            Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))

            GlassButton(
                text = skipLabel,
                onClick = onSkip,
                primary = false,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .focusRequester(skipFocus)
                    .focusProperties { up = googleFocus },
            )
        }
    }
}

                            onClick = { vm.onAccountTypeChange("M3U") },
