                    if (state.accountType == "M3U") {
                        LabeledInput(
                            label = "رابط M3U المباشر",
                            dot = Palette.Glow,
                            value = state.server,
                            onValueChange = vm::onServer,
                            placeholder = "http://example.com/playlist.m3u",
                            icon = Ic.Link,
                            keyboardType = KeyboardType.Uri,
                            imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                            onImeAction = { vm.submit(onLoggedIn) },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                        )
                    } else {
                        
                    if (wide) {
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp),
                        ) {
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.serverLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Glow,
                                value = state.server,
                                onValueChange = vm::onServer,
                                placeholder = "http://portal.server.com:8080",
                                icon = Ic.Link,
                                keyboardType = KeyboardType.Uri,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Next,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            )
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.userLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Royal,
                                value = state.user,
                                onValueChange = vm::onUser,
                                placeholder = Ar.userPlaceholder,
                                icon = Ic.Person,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Next,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            )
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.passLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Gold,
                                value = state.pass,
                                onValueChange = vm::onPass,
                                placeholder = "••••••••",
                                icon = Ic.Lock,
                                password = true,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                                onImeAction = { vm.submit(onLoggedIn) },
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.serverLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Glow,
                                value = state.server,
                                onValueChange = vm::onServer,
                                placeholder = "http://portal.server.com:8080",
                                icon = Ic.Link,
                                keyboardType = KeyboardType.Uri,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Next,
                            )
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.userLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Royal,
                                value = state.user,
                                onValueChange = vm::onUser,
                                placeholder = Ar.userPlaceholder,
                                icon = Ic.Person,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Next,
                            )
                            LabeledInput(
                                label = Ar.passLabel,
                                dot = Palette.Gold,
                                value = state.pass,
                                onValueChange = vm::onPass,
                                placeholder = "••••••••",
                                icon = Ic.Lock,
                                password = true,
                                imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                                onImeAction = { vm.submit(onLoggedIn) },
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    
                    }

                    val trimmedServer = state.server.trim()
                    val isInsecureHttp = trimmedServer.isNotEmpty() &&
                        !trimmedServer.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true)
                    if (isInsecureHttp) {
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                        HttpSecurityWarningBanner()
                    }

                    val error = state.error
                    if (error != null) {
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                        ErrorBanner(error)
                    }

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(24.dp))

                    // Action bar: M3U tip card on the right (RTL start), primary submit button on the left (RTL end).
                    if (wide) {
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        ) {
                            M3uTipCard()
                            SubmitControl(state.loading, isTv) { vm.submit(onLoggedIn) }
                        }
                    } else {
                        Column(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        ) {
                            SubmitControl(state.loading, isTv, Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) { vm.submit(onLoggedIn) }
                            M3uTipCard(Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))

            // Bottom status & quick options bar
            LoginFooter(
                loading = state.loading,
                hasError = state.error != null,
                light = light,
                onToggleLight = { scope.launch { container.prefs.setLightEffects(!light) } },
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LabeledInput(
    label: String,
    dot: Color,
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    placeholder: String,
    icon: ImageVector,

) {

        GlassTextField(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            placeholder = placeholder,
            leading = icon,
            keyboardType = keyboardType,
            imeAction = imeAction,
            password = password,
            ltr = true,
            onImeAction = onImeAction,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SubmitControl(loading: Boolean, isTv: Boolean, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, onSubmit: () -> Unit) {
    if (loading) {
        Box(modifier.padding(horizontal = 24.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            CircularProgressIndicator(color = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp))
        }
    } else {
        GlassButton(
            text = Ar.loginInstall,
            onClick = onSubmit,
            modifier = modifier,
            primary = true,
            autoFocus = isTv,
            trailingIcon = Ic.ArrowLeft,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LoginFooter(
    loading: Boolean,
    hasError: Boolean,
    light: Boolean,
    onToggleLight: () -> Unit,
) {
    val dotColor = when {
        hasError -> Palette.Live
        loading -> Palette.Gold
        else -> Palette.Emerald
    }
    val statusText = when {
        hasError -> Ar.statusFailed
        loading -> Ar.statusConnecting
        else -> Ar.statusReady
    }
    Row(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
            .background(Palette.Glass)
            .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
    ) {
        DotTitle(statusText, dotColor, fontSize = 13.sp, color = dotColor)
        GlassCard(
            onClick = onToggleLight,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
            selected = light,
            focusScale = 1.03f,
        ) {
            Row(
                Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Icon(Ic.Settings, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Royal, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(Ar.lightMode, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ErrorBanner(message: String) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(Palette.Live.copy(alpha = 0.16f))
            .border(1.dp, Palette.Live.copy(alpha = 0.45f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {

                .size(8.dp)

                .background(Palette.Live),
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
        Text(
            text = message,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            color = Palette.Text,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
        )
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/login/LoginViewModel.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.login

import android.net.Uri
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.saydlawy.sahab.AppContainer
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.Account
import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.Ar
import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.ServerUrl
import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.userMessage
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class LoginUiState(
    val accountType: String = "XTREAM",
    val server: String = "",
    val user: String = "",
    val pass: String = "",
    val loading: Boolean = false,
    val error: String? = null,
)

class LoginViewModel(private val container: AppContainer) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(LoginUiState())
    val state = _state.asStateFlow()

    fun onAccountTypeChange(type: String) {
        _state.update { it.copy(accountType = type, error = null) }
    }

    fun onServer(value: String) {
        // A full playlist link (…/get.php?username=…&password=…) fills in all three fields at once.
        val parsed = if (_state.value.accountType == "XTREAM" && value.contains("username=") && value.contains("password=")) {
            runCatching {
                val uri = Uri.parse(value.trim())
                Pair(uri.getQueryParameter("username").orEmpty(), uri.getQueryParameter("password").orEmpty())
            }.getOrNull()
        } else null
        _state.update {
            if (parsed != null && parsed.first.isNotBlank()) {
                it.copy(server = value, user = parsed.first, pass = parsed.second, error = null)
            } else {
                it.copy(server = value, error = null)
            }
        }
    }

    fun onUser(value: String) = _state.update { it.copy(user = value, error = null) }

    fun onPass(value: String) = _state.update { it.copy(pass = value, error = null) }

    fun submit(onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        val current = _state.value
        if (current.loading) return

        if (current.accountType == "M3U") {
            val url = current.server.trim()
            if (url.isBlank() || (!url.startsWith("http://") && !url.startsWith("https://"))) {
                _state.update { it.copy(error = "الرجاء إدخال رابط M3U صالح يبدأ بـ http") }
                return
            }
            _state.update { it.copy(loading = true, error = null) }
            viewModelScope.launch {
                try {
                    val account = Account(name = "M3U Playlist", type = "M3U", m3uUrl = url)
                    container.repo.login(account)
                    container.prefs.saveAccount(account)
                    container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(container.prefs.getAccounts())
                    _state.update { it.copy(loading = false) }
                    onSuccess()
                } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                    throw e
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, error = e.userMessage()) }
                }
            }
            return
        }

        val server = ServerUrl.normalizeServer(current.server)
        if (server == null) {
            _state.update { it.copy(error = Ar.errBadUrl) }
            return
        }
        if (current.user.isBlank() || current.pass.isBlank()) {
            _state.update { it.copy(error = Ar.errFillAll) }
            return
        }
        _state.update { it.copy(loading = true, error = null) }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val account = Account(
                    name = current.user.trim().ifBlank { "My TV" },
                    type = "XTREAM",
                    server = server,
                    user = current.user.trim(),
                    pass = current.pass.trim(),
                )
                container.repo.login(account)
                container.prefs.saveAccount(account)
                // حفظ في السحابة إذا كان المستخدم مسجل دخول
                container.userSyncService.saveXtreamAccount(account)
                container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(container.prefs.getAccounts())
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false) }
                onSuccess()
            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _state.update { it.copy(loading = false, error = e.userMessage()) }
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/LiveVideoStallWatchdog.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import androidx.annotation.OptIn
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.rememberUpdatedState
import androidx.media3.common.Player
import androidx.media3.common.util.UnstableApi
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive

/**
 * LIVE-01: Detects the "frozen picture while audio keeps playing" deadlock.
 *
 * Some hardware video decoders (observed on Amlogic `OMX.amlogic.*.decoder.awesome2` on the TCL TV)
 * silently stop returning output buffers. No [androidx.media3.common.PlaybackException] is thrown,
 * so `onPlayerError` never fires, while the audio renderer (the master clock) keeps advancing.
 *
 * The watchdog samples the video decoder's rendered-frame counter once per second. When the player
 * is READY + playing, a video track is active, no frame has been rendered for [STALL_MS] and the
 * playback position (audio clock) advanced at least [MIN_AUDIO_ADVANCE_MS], it recovers:
 *  1. `stop()` (releases the stuck codec) + `seekToDefaultPosition()` + `prepare()` at the live edge.
 *  2. From the second stall onwards, [onEscalate] may switch the stream container (m3u8 <-> ts).
 * At most [MAX_RECOVERIES] recoveries per [RECOVERY_WINDOW_MS] per item, so still-image / very
 * low-fps channels can never cause a reset loop.
 */
@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
@Composable
fun LiveVideoStallWatchdog(
    player: ExoPlayer?,
    enabled: Boolean,
    itemKey: Any?,
    onEscalate: () -> Boolean = { false },
) {
    val escalate by rememberUpdatedState(onEscalate)
    LaunchedEffect(player, enabled, itemKey) {
        if (player == null || !enabled) return@LaunchedEffect
        var lastRendered = -1
        var frozenSinceMs = 0L
        var positionAtFreezeMs = 0L
        var recoveries = 0
        var windowStartMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()

        while (isActive) {
            delay(SAMPLE_MS)
            val counters = player.videoDecoderCounters
            if (counters == null) {
                lastRendered = -1
                continue
            }

            val armed = player.isPlaying &&
                player.playbackState == Player.STATE_READY &&
                player.videoFormat != null
            if (!armed) {
                lastRendered = -1
                continue
            }

            counters.ensureUpdated()
            val rendered = counters.renderedOutputBufferCount
            val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
            val position = player.currentPosition
            if (rendered != lastRendered) {
                lastRendered = rendered
                frozenSinceMs = now
                positionAtFreezeMs = position
                continue
            }

            val frozenFor = now - frozenSinceMs
            val audioAdvanced = position - positionAtFreezeMs
            if (frozenFor < STALL_MS || audioAdvanced < MIN_AUDIO_ADVANCE_MS) continue

            if (now - windowStartMs > RECOVERY_WINDOW_MS) {
                windowStartMs = now
                recoveries = 0
            }
            if (recoveries >= MAX_RECOVERIES) {
                // Give up for this item: probably a still-image / ultra low-fps channel.
                frozenSinceMs = now
                positionAtFreezeMs = position
                continue
            }
            recoveries++
            val stallMsg = "Live video stall: no frame for ${frozenFor}ms while audio advanced " +
                "${audioAdvanced}ms (rendered=$rendered, dropped=${counters.droppedBufferCount}) " +
                "-> recovery #$recoveries"
            Log.w(TAG, stallMsg)
            runCatching { com.google.firebase.crashlytics.FirebaseCrashlytics.getInstance().log(stallMsg) }
            // stop() resets the renderers, which releases the deadlocked MediaCodec instance.
            player.stop()
            val escalated = recoveries >= 2 && escalate()
            if (!escalated) {
                player.seekToDefaultPosition()
                player.prepare()
                player.play()
            }
            lastRendered = -1
        }
    }
}

private const val TAG = "SahabLiveWatchdog"
private const val SAMPLE_MS = 1_000L
private const val STALL_MS = 4_500L
private const val MIN_AUDIO_ADVANCE_MS = 3_000L
private const val MAX_RECOVERIES = 3
private const val RECOVERY_WINDOW_MS = 120_000L

// Design tokens from ExoPlayer/DESIGN.md & media-player/DESIGN.md
@Composable
internal fun LiveChannelsDrawer(
    playlist: List<PlayItem>,
    currentIndex: Int,
    currentProgram: Program?,
    compact: Boolean = false,
    drawerFocus: FocusRequester,
    onSelectChannel: (Int) -> Unit,
    onClose: () -> Unit,
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .width(if (compact) 320.dp else 360.dp)
            .fillMaxHeight()
            .background(OsdSurfaceLowest.copy(alpha = 0.90f))
            .border(1.dp, OsdOutlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.40f), RoundedCornerShape(topEnd = 20.dp, bottomEnd = 20.dp))
            .padding(14.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            ) {
                Icon(Ic.List, contentDescription = null, tint = OsdPrimary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                Text(
                    "قائمة القنوات (${currentIndex + 1}/${playlist.size})",
                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = Color.White,
                )
            }
            OsdIconSquareButton(Ic.Close, Ar.close, onClose, size = 34.dp, circle = true)
        }

        val channelListState = rememberLazyListState()
        LaunchedEffect(currentIndex) {
            if (currentIndex > 0) {
                channelListState.scrollToItem((currentIndex - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
            }
        }

        LazyColumn(
            state = channelListState,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                    if (event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown && (event.key == Key.Back || event.key == Key.Escape)) {
                        onClose()
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                },
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        ) {
            val anchorIdx = currentIndex.takeIf { it in playlist.indices } ?: 0
            itemsIndexed(
                playlist,
                key = { idx, c -> "${c.id}_$idx" },
                contentType = { _, _ -> "drawer_channel_row" },
            ) { idx, ch ->
                val active = idx == currentIndex
                val isFocusAnchor = idx == anchorIdx
                var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .then(if (isFocusAnchor) Modifier.focusRequester(drawerFocus) else Modifier)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(
                            when {
                                active -> OsdPrimaryContainer.copy(alpha = 0.42f)
                                focused -> OsdSurfaceHighest.copy(alpha = 0.92f)
                                else -> OsdSurfaceHigh.copy(alpha = 0.70f)
                            },
                        )
                        .border(
                            width = if (active || focused) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                            color = if (active || focused) OsdCyan else Color.Transparent,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        )
                        .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused }
                        .clickable { onSelectChannel(idx) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                ) {
                    Text(
                        String.format(Locale.US, "%02d", idx + 1),
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = if (active) OsdCyan else OsdOnSurfaceVariant,
                    )
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            ch.title,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = OsdOnSurface,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        )
                        if (active && currentProgram != null) {
                            Text(
                                currentProgram.title,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = OsdCyan,
                                maxLines = 1,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    if (active) {
                        Icon(Ic.Equalizer, contentDescription = null, tint = OsdCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// 3. SIDE EPISODES DRAWER (Matches media-player/screen.png & code.html)
// ============================================================================

@Composable
internal fun VodEpisodesDrawer(
    currentItem: PlayItem,
    playlist: List<PlayItem>,
    currentIndex: Int,
    seriesDetails: SeriesDetails?,
    progressFlow: StateFlow<PlaybackProgress>,
    compact: Boolean = false,
    drawerFocus: FocusRequester,
    onSelectPlaylistIndex: (Int) -> Unit,
    onSelectEpisode: (Episode) -> Unit,
    onClose: () -> Unit,
) {
    val allEpisodes = seriesDetails?.episodes.orEmpty()
    val seasons = remember(seriesDetails) { seriesDetails?.seasons.orEmpty() }
    val currentEpisodeObj = remember(allEpisodes, currentItem.id) {
        allEpisodes.firstOrNull { it.id == currentItem.id }
    }
    var selectedSeason by remember(currentEpisodeObj, seasons) {
        mutableIntStateOf(currentEpisodeObj?.season ?: seasons.firstOrNull() ?: 1)
    }

    val seasonEpisodes = remember(allEpisodes, selectedSeason) {
        if (allEpisodes.isEmpty()) emptyList()
        else allEpisodes.filter { it.season == selectedSeason }
    }

    val totalCount = if (allEpisodes.isNotEmpty()) allEpisodes.size else playlist.size

    val episodeListState = rememberLazyListState()
    LaunchedEffect(selectedSeason, currentItem.id, currentIndex) {
        val targetIndex = if (seasonEpisodes.isNotEmpty()) {
            seasonEpisodes.indexOfFirst { it.id == currentItem.id }.takeIf { it >= 0 } ?: 0
        } else {
            currentIndex.coerceIn(0, (playlist.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        }
        if (targetIndex > 0) {
            episodeListState.scrollToItem((targetIndex - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .width(if (compact) 320.dp else 380.dp)
            .widthIn(max = 400.dp)
            .fillMaxHeight()
            .background(OsdSurfaceLowest.copy(alpha = 0.90f))
            .border(1.dp, OsdOutlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.40f), RoundedCornerShape(topEnd = 20.dp, bottomEnd = 20.dp))
            .padding(if (compact) 14.dp else 18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(if (compact) 10.dp else 12.dp),
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                Icon(Ic.Series, contentDescription = null, tint = OsdPrimary, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                Column {
                    Text(
                        if (currentItem.kind == Kind.EPISODE) "حلقات المسلسل" else "قائمة التشغيل",
                        fontSize = if (compact) 16.sp else 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Color.White,
                    )
                    Text(
                        "${currentItem.title} • $totalCount حلقات",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = OsdOutline,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
            }
            OsdIconSquareButton(
                icon = Ic.Close,
                description = Ar.close,
                onClick = onClose,
                size = 36.dp,
                circle = true,
            )
        }

        if (seasons.isNotEmpty()) {
            LazyRow(
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 2.dp),
            ) {
                items(seasons, key = { it }) { seasonNum ->
                    val selected = seasonNum == selectedSeason
                    var seasonFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(if (selected) OsdPrimary else OsdSurfaceHigh.copy(alpha = 0.80f))
                            .onFocusChanged { seasonFocused = it.isFocused }
                            .border(
                                width = if (seasonFocused) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                                color = if (seasonFocused) OsdCyan else Color.Transparent,
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                            )
                            .clickable { selectedSeason = seasonNum }
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 7.dp),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            Ar.season(seasonNum),
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = if (selected) OsdOnPrimary else OsdOnSurface,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        LazyColumn(
            state = episodeListState,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                    if (event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown && (event.key == Key.Back || event.key == Key.Escape)) {
                        onClose()
                        true
                    } else {
                        false
                    }
                },
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            if (seasonEpisodes.isNotEmpty()) {
                val anchorEpIdx = seasonEpisodes.indexOfFirst { it.id == currentItem.id }.let { if (it >= 0) it else 0 }
                itemsIndexed(seasonEpisodes, key = { _, ep -> ep.id }) { epIdx, ep ->
                    val isCurrent = ep.id == currentItem.id
                    val isFocusAnchor = epIdx == anchorEpIdx
                    val isWatched = currentEpisodeObj != null &&
                        (ep.season < currentEpisodeObj.season ||
                            (ep.season == currentEpisodeObj.season && ep.number < currentEpisodeObj.number))
                    val isNext = currentEpisodeObj != null &&
                        ep.season == currentEpisodeObj.season &&
                        ep.number == currentEpisodeObj.number + 1
                    val progState = if (isCurrent) progressFlow.collectAsStateWithLifecycle().value else null
                    val curPos = progState?.position ?: 0L
                    val curDur = progState?.duration ?: 0L

                    EpisodeDrawerCard(
                        number = ep.number,
                        title = ep.title,
                        plot = ep.plot.ifBlank { "حلقة كاملة بجودة عالية من ${currentItem.title}" },
                        imageUrl = ep.image.ifBlank { seriesDetails?.cover.orEmpty() }.ifBlank { currentItem.icon },
                        durationText = ep.duration,
                        isCurrent = isCurrent,
                        isWatched = isWatched,
                        isNext = isNext,
                        progressFraction = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) (curPos.toFloat() / curDur).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) else if (isWatched) 1f else 0f,
                        remainingMinutes = if (isCurrent && curDur > curPos) ((curDur - curPos) / 60_000L).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1) else 0,
                        modifier = if (isFocusAnchor) Modifier.focusRequester(drawerFocus) else Modifier,
                        onClick = { onSelectEpisode(ep) },
                    )
                }
            } else {
                val anchorPlayIdx = currentIndex.takeIf { it in playlist.indices } ?: 0
                itemsIndexed(playlist, key = { i, p -> "${p.id}_$i" }) { idx, p ->
                    val isCurrent = idx == currentIndex
                    val isFocusAnchor = idx == anchorPlayIdx
                    val isWatched = idx < currentIndex
                    val isNext = idx == currentIndex + 1
                    val progState = if (isCurrent) progressFlow.collectAsStateWithLifecycle().value else null
                    val curPos = progState?.position ?: 0L
                    val curDur = progState?.duration ?: 0L

                    EpisodeDrawerCard(
                        number = idx + 1,
                        title = p.subtitle.ifBlank { p.title },
                        plot = p.title,
                        imageUrl = p.icon,
                        durationText = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) formatDuration(curDur) else "",
                        isCurrent = isCurrent,
                        isWatched = isWatched,
                        isNext = isNext,
                        progressFraction = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) (curPos.toFloat() / curDur).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) else if (isWatched) 1f else 0f,
                        remainingMinutes = if (isCurrent && curDur > curPos) ((curDur - curPos) / 60_000L).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1) else 0,
                        modifier = if (isFocusAnchor) Modifier.focusRequester(drawerFocus) else Modifier,
                        onClick = { onSelectPlaylistIndex(idx) },
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun EpisodeDrawerCard(
    number: Int,
    title: String,
    plot: String,
    imageUrl: String,
    durationText: String,
    isCurrent: Boolean,
    isWatched: Boolean,
    isNext: Boolean,
    progressFraction: Float,
    remainingMinutes: Int,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val bg = when {
        isCurrent -> OsdPrimaryContainer.copy(alpha = 0.24f)
        focused -> OsdSurfaceHigh.copy(alpha = 0.90f)
        else -> OsdSurfaceContainer.copy(alpha = 0.65f)
    }
    val borderColor = when {
        isCurrent || focused -> OsdPrimary
        else -> Color.Transparent
    }

    Box(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(bg)
                .border(if (isCurrent || focused) 2.dp else 1.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused }
                .clickable(onClick = onClick)
                .padding(10.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .width(114.dp)
                    .height(70.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                    .background(OsdSurfaceLowest),
            ) {
                RemoteImage(
                    url = imageUrl,
                    fallbackText = title,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                )
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(
                            when {
                                isCurrent -> OsdPrimaryContainer.copy(alpha = 0.42f)
                                isWatched -> OsdSurfaceLowest.copy(alpha = 0.60f)
                                else -> OsdSurfaceLowest.copy(alpha = 0.30f)
                            },
                        ),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    val overlayIcon = when {
                        isCurrent -> Ic.VolumeUp
                        isWatched -> Ic.CheckCircle
                        else -> Ic.Play
                    }
                    val overlayTint = if (isWatched) OsdCyan else Color.White
                    Icon(overlayIcon, contentDescription = null, tint = overlayTint, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                }

                if (isCurrent) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(4.dp)
                            .background(OsdSurfaceHighest),
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxHeight()
                                .fillMaxWidth(progressFraction.coerceIn(0.05f, 1f))
                                .background(OsdCyan),
                        )
                    }
                } else if (durationText.isNotBlank()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                            .padding(4.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .background(OsdSurfaceLowest.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                    ) {
                        Text(durationText, fontSize = 10.sp, color = OsdOnSurface)
                    }
                }
            }

            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp),
            ) {
                when {
                    isCurrent -> Text(
                        "الحلقة الحالية قيد المشاهدة",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = OsdPrimary,
                    )
                    isWatched -> Text(
                        "تمت المشاهدة بالكامل",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = OsdCyan,
                    )
                    isNext -> Text(
                        "الحلقة التالية",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = OsdPrimary,
                    )
                }

                Text(
                    "$number. $title",
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = if (isCurrent) Color.White else OsdOnSurface,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )

                Text(
                    plot,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = if (isCurrent) OsdTertiary else OsdOutline,
                    maxLines = 2,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )

                if (isCurrent && remainingMinutes > 0) {
                    Text("متبقي $remainingMinutes دقيقة", fontSize = 11.sp, color = OsdPrimary)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.ChannelLogoResolver

@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
@Composable
internal fun PlayerContent(container: AppContainer, request: PlayRequest, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val view = LocalView.current
    val isTv = LocalIsTv.current
    val displayMode = LocalDisplayMode.current
    val vm: PlayerViewModel = appViewModel { PlayerViewModel(it, request) }
    val ui by vm.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    val playlist = ui.playlist
    val index = ui.index
    val item = ui.currentItem
    val isLive = ui.isLive
    val isPlaying = ui.isPlaying
    val buffering = ui.buffering
    val reconnecting = ui.reconnecting
    val error = ui.error
    val playbackSpeed = ui.playbackSpeed
    val resizeIndex = ui.resizeIndex
    val seriesDetails = ui.seriesDetails
    val requestPlayFocusTick = ui.requestPlayFocusTick

    val exo = remember { SahabPlayer.create(context, container.client, isLive = isLive, isPreview = false, accountHash = container.accountHash) }

    var tracks by remember { mutableStateOf(Tracks.EMPTY) }
    var trackSelectionParams by remember { mutableStateOf(exo.trackSelectionParameters) }
    var controlsVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var interactions by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var showTracks by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showGuide by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showQuickChannelsDrawer by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showEpisodesDrawer by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var ended by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var stoppedByLifecycle by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var mediaLive by remember(item.id) { mutableStateOf(isLive) }
    var mediaSeekable by remember(item.id) { mutableStateOf(!isLive) }
    val canSeek = !isLive && !mediaLive && mediaSeekable
    val rootFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val playFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val drawerFocus = remember { FocusRequester() }

    LaunchedEffect(item.groupId, item.kind) {
        vm.loadSeriesIfNeeded(item)
    }

    val saveNow: () -> Unit = {
        val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
        vm.saveProgress(exo.currentPosition, total)
    }

    var isPlayerReleased by remember(exo) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val releasePlayerNow: () -> Unit = {
        if (!isPlayerReleased) {
            isPlayerReleased = true
            runCatching { saveNow() }
            runCatching { vm.flushRecents() }
            runCatching { SahabMediaSessionBridge.detach(context, exo) }
            SahabPlayer.releasePlayer(exo)
        }
    }
    val exitPlayerNow: () -> Unit = {
        releasePlayerNow()
        onBack()
    }

    val goTo: (Int) -> Unit = { target ->
        val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
        if (vm.goTo(target, exo.currentPosition, total)) {
            ended = false
            controlsVisible = true
            interactions++
        }
    }

    val playEpisodeFromDrawer: (Episode) -> Unit = { ep ->
        val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
        if (vm.playEpisodeFromDrawer(ep, exo.currentPosition, total)) {
            ended = false
            controlsVisible = true
            interactions++
        }
    }

    val zapNext: () -> Unit = {
        val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
        if (vm.zapNext(exo.currentPosition, total)) {
            ended = false
            controlsVisible = true
            interactions++
        }
    }
    val zapPrevious: () -> Unit = {
        val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
        if (vm.zapPrevious(exo.currentPosition, total)) {
            ended = false
            controlsVisible = true
            interactions++
        }
    }
    val togglePlay: () -> Unit = {
        if (exo.playbackState == Player.STATE_ENDED) {
            // Only restart on explicit manual user play press — never auto-repeat
            exo.seekTo(0L)
            exo.play()
        } else if (exo.isPlaying) {
            exo.pause()
        } else {
            exo.play()
        }
        interactions++
    }
    val seekBy: (Long) -> Unit = { delta ->
        if (canSeek) {
            val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET && it > 0 } ?: Long.MAX_VALUE
            exo.seekTo((exo.currentPosition + delta).coerceIn(0L, total))
            vm.onSeekPosition(exo.currentPosition)
            interactions++
        }
    }
    val cycleSpeed: () -> Unit = {
        if (canSeek) {
            val nextSpeed = vm.cycleSpeed(PlaybackSpeeds)
            exo.setPlaybackSpeed(nextSpeed)
            interactions++
        }
    }

    val toggleOrientation: () -> Unit = {
        val activity = context.findActivity()
        if (activity != null && !isTv) {
            activity.requestedOrientation = if (displayMode == DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) {
                ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
            } else {
                ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT
            }
        }
    }

    // Keep screen awake; hide system bars only in landscape / TV mode so portrait phone has normal status bar spacing.
    DisposableEffect(displayMode) {
        val activity = context.findActivity()
        val window = activity?.window
        val controller = window?.let { WindowCompat.getInsetsController(it, view) }
        if (displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) {
            controller?.systemBarsBehavior = WindowInsetsControllerCompat.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE
            controller?.hide(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
        } else {
            controller?.show(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
        }
        view.keepScreenOn = true
        onDispose {
            controller?.show(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
            view.keepScreenOn = false
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        val activity = context.findActivity()
        val previousOrientation = activity?.requestedOrientation
        onDispose {
            if (!isTv && activity != null) {
                activity.requestedOrientation = previousOrientation ?: ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_UNSPECIFIED
            }
            releasePlayerNow()
        }
    }

    val isInPipMode by SahabMediaSessionBridge.inPipMode.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    LaunchedEffect(isTv, isPlaying) {
        SahabMediaSessionBridge.pipAllowed = !isTv && (isPlaying || exo.playWhenReady)
    }

    LifecycleEventEffect(Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP) {
        saveNow()
        vm.flushRecents()
        val inPip = context.findActivity()?.isInPictureInPictureMode == true
        // O-03: keep live audio (MediaSessionService) or phone PiP running in background;
        // on TV or VOD outside PiP, pause normally.
        if (!inPip && !(isLive && !isTv) && (exo.playWhenReady || exo.isPlaying)) {
            stoppedByLifecycle = true
            exo.pause()
        }
    }

    LifecycleEventEffect(Lifecycle.Event.ON_START) {
        if (stoppedByLifecycle) {
            stoppedByLifecycle = false
            if (isLive) {
                exo.seekToDefaultPosition()
                exo.prepare()
            }
            exo.play()
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(exo) {
        // Lock player software volume at 1.0f (unity gain) & disable looping permanently
        exo.volume = 1.0f
        exo.repeatMode = Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF
        val listener = object : Player.Listener {
            override fun onIsPlayingChanged(playing: Boolean) {
                vm.onIsPlayingChanged(playing)
                if (!playing && exo.playbackState != Player.STATE_IDLE) {
                    val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
                    vm.onPlaybackPausedOrStopped(exo.currentPosition, total)
                }
            }

            override fun onPlaybackStateChanged(state: Int) {
                vm.onBufferingReady(state == Player.STATE_BUFFERING, state == Player.STATE_READY)
                if (!exo.currentTimeline.isEmpty || state == Player.STATE_READY) {
                    val liveNow = isLive || exo.isCurrentMediaItemLive
                    mediaLive = liveNow
                    mediaSeekable = !liveNow && exo.isCurrentMediaItemSeekable
                }
                if (state == Player.STATE_ENDED) ended = true
            }

            override fun onTimelineChanged(timeline: Timeline, reason: Int) {
                if (!timeline.isEmpty) {
                    val liveNow = isLive || exo.isCurrentMediaItemLive
                    mediaLive = liveNow
                    mediaSeekable = !liveNow && exo.isCurrentMediaItemSeekable
                }
            }

            override fun onPlayerError(e: PlaybackException) {
                vm.onPlayerError(e.errorCodeName, extractHttpResponseCode(e))
            }

            override fun onTracksChanged(newTracks: Tracks) {
                tracks = newTracks
            }

            override fun onTrackSelectionParametersChanged(parameters: TrackSelectionParameters) {
                trackSelectionParams = parameters
            }
        }
        exo.addListener(listener)
        SahabMediaSessionBridge.attach(context, exo, allowBackgroundService = isLive && !isTv)
        onDispose {
            SahabMediaSessionBridge.detach(context, exo)
            exo.removeListener(listener)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(item.id, ui.retryKey, ui.formatAttempt, ui.autoReconnectCount) {
        val activeItem = item
        vm.onPrepareStarted()
        if (ui.autoReconnectCount > 0) {
            delay(1_100)
        }
        val url = try {
            vm.streamUrl(activeItem, ui.formatAttempt)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            vm.onUrlError()
            return@LaunchedEffect
        }
        val resume = vm.resolveResumePositionMs(activeItem, exo.currentPosition)
        exo.volume = 1.0f
        exo.repeatMode = Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF
        val metadata = MediaMetadata.Builder()
            .setTitle(activeItem.title)
            .setSubtitle(activeItem.subtitle.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() })
            .setArtworkUri(activeItem.icon.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let(android.net.Uri::parse))
            .build()
        val mediaItem = MediaItem.Builder()
            .setUri(url)
            .setMediaMetadata(metadata)
            .build()
        exo.setMediaItem(mediaItem, resume)
        exo.prepare()
        exo.playWhenReady = true
    }

    // LIVE-01: recover from silent hardware-decoder stalls (frozen picture while audio continues).
    LiveVideoStallWatchdog(
        player = exo,
        enabled = isLive,
        itemKey = item.id,
        onEscalate = { vm.switchLiveFormatAfterStall() },
    )

    LaunchedEffect(ended) {
        if (ended) {
            ended = false
            val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET && it > 0 } ?: 0L
            vm.onPlaybackEnded(total) {
                // Last episode or movie ended: NEVER auto-repeat/loop same item
                exo.playWhenReady = false
                exo.pause()
                controlsVisible = true
                vm.bumpPlayFocusTick()
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(exo, isPlayerReleased, controlsVisible, showEpisodesDrawer, displayMode) {
        if (isPlayerReleased) return@LaunchedEffect
        val shouldTickUi = controlsVisible || showEpisodesDrawer || displayMode == DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT
        if (!shouldTickUi) return@LaunchedEffect
        while (!isPlayerReleased) {
            val cur = exo.currentPosition.coerceAtLeast(0L)
            val buf = exo.bufferedPosition.coerceAtLeast(cur)
            val dur = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET && it > 0 } ?: 0L
            vm.onProgressUpdate(cur, buf, dur)
            delay(500)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(exo, isPlayerReleased, isLive, isPlaying) {
        if (isPlayerReleased || isLive || !isPlaying) return@LaunchedEffect
        while (!isPlayerReleased && isPlaying) {
            delay(30_000L)
            if (isPlayerReleased || !exo.isPlaying) break
            val total = exo.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: 0L
            vm.saveProgressIfVod(exo.currentPosition, total)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(
        controlsVisible,
        interactions,
        isPlaying,
        showTracks,
        showGuide,
        showEpisodesDrawer,
        showQuickChannelsDrawer,
    ) {
        if (controlsVisible && isPlaying && !showTracks && !showGuide && !showEpisodesDrawer && !showQuickChannelsDrawer) {
            delay(CONTROLS_HIDE_MS)
            controlsVisible = false
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(controlsVisible, requestPlayFocusTick, showEpisodesDrawer, showQuickChannelsDrawer) {
        if (showEpisodesDrawer || (showQuickChannelsDrawer && isLive)) {
            delay(60)
            withFrameNanos { }
            runCatching { drawerFocus.requestFocus() }
        } else if (controlsVisible && displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) {
            delay(50)
            withFrameNanos { }
            runCatching { playFocus.requestFocus() }
        } else if (!controlsVisible && !showEpisodesDrawer && !showQuickChannelsDrawer) {
            delay(60)
            withFrameNanos { }
            runCatching { rootFocus.requestFocus() }
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(80)
        withFrameNanos { }
        runCatching {
            if (controlsVisible && displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) playFocus.requestFocus()
            else rootFocus.requestFocus()
        }
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = true) {
        when {
            showEpisodesDrawer -> showEpisodesDrawer = false
            showQuickChannelsDrawer && isLive -> showQuickChannelsDrawer = false
            controlsVisible && displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT -> controlsVisible = false
            else -> exitPlayerNow()
        }
    }

    val onKey: (KeyEvent) -> Boolean = handler@{ event ->
        if (event.type != KeyEventType.KeyDown) return@handler false
        // Preserve hardware/source volume: never intercept or alter volume internally
        if (event.key == Key.VolumeUp || event.key == Key.VolumeDown || event.key == Key.VolumeMute) {
            return@handler false
        }
        interactions++
        when (event.key) {
            Key.MediaPlayPause -> {
                togglePlay()
                controlsVisible = true
                vm.bumpPlayFocusTick()
                true
            }
            Key.MediaPlay -> {
                exo.play()
                true
            }
            Key.MediaPause -> {
                exo.pause()
                controlsVisible = true
                vm.bumpPlayFocusTick()
                true
            }
            Key.MediaFastForward -> {
                if (canSeek) seekBy(3 * SEEK_STEP_MS)
                true
            }
            Key.MediaRewind -> {
                if (canSeek) seekBy(-3 * SEEK_STEP_MS)
                true
            }
            Key.MediaNext, Key.ChannelUp -> {
                zapNext()
                true
            }
            Key.MediaPrevious, Key.ChannelDown -> {
                zapPrevious()
                true
            }
            Key.Back, Key.Escape -> {
                if (showEpisodesDrawer) {
                    showEpisodesDrawer = false
                    true
                } else if (showQuickChannelsDrawer && isLive) {
                    showQuickChannelsDrawer = false
                    true
                } else if (controlsVisible && displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) {
                    controlsVisible = false
                    true
                } else {
                    exitPlayerNow()
                    true
                }
            }
            else -> {
                if (controlsVisible || showEpisodesDrawer || (showQuickChannelsDrawer && isLive)) {
                    false
                } else {
                    when (event.key) {
                        Key.DirectionCenter, Key.Enter, Key.Spacebar -> {
                            // First OK pauses playback, shows OSD, and highlights Play/Pause button with cyan glow
                            if (exo.isPlaying) {
                                exo.pause()
                            } else if (exo.playbackState == Player.STATE_ENDED) {
                                exo.seekTo(0L)
                                exo.play()
                            } else {
                                exo.play()
                            }
                            controlsVisible = true
                            vm.bumpPlayFocusTick()
                        }
                        Key.DirectionUp -> {
                            if (isLive) zapNext() else if (playlist.size > 1 || seriesDetails != null) {
                                showEpisodesDrawer = true
                            }
                            controlsVisible = true
                        }
                        Key.DirectionDown -> {
                            if (isLive) zapPrevious() else {
                                showTracks = true
                            }
                            controlsVisible = true
                        }
                        Key.DirectionRight -> {
                            if (canSeek) seekBy(SEEK_STEP_MS)
                            controlsVisible = true
                        }
                        Key.DirectionLeft -> {
                            if (canSeek) seekBy(-SEEK_STEP_MS)
                            controlsVisible = true
                        }
                        else -> controlsVisible = true
                    }
                    true
                }
            }
        }
    }

    val dao = remember(container) { container.channelDb.channelDao() }
    val livePrograms by produceState<Pair<EpgProgramEntity?, EpgProgramEntity?>>(
        initialValue = null to null,
        item.id,
        isLive,
    ) {
        if (!isLive) {
            value = null to null
            return@produceState
        }
        while (true) {
            val t = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000
            val cur = runCatching { dao.currentProgramFor(item.id, t) }.getOrNull()
            val nxt = runCatching { dao.nextProgramFor(item.id, t) }.getOrNull()
            value = cur to nxt
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(60_000L)
        }
    }
    val currentProgram = livePrograms.first?.let { Program(it.title, it.description, it.start, it.end) }
    val nextProgram = livePrograms.second?.let { Program(it.title, it.description, it.start, it.end) }

    val textOff = remember(tracks, trackSelectionParams) {
        trackSelectionParams.disabledTrackTypes.contains(C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT)
    }
    val hasVideoOverride = remember(tracks, trackSelectionParams) {
        tracks.groups.any { g ->
            g.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && trackSelectionParams.overrides.containsKey(g.mediaTrackGroup)
        }
    }
    val activeBitrateText = remember(tracks) {
        val selectedFormats = tracks.groups.asSequence()
            .filter { it.isSelected && (it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO || it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO) }
            .flatMap { group ->
                (0 until group.length).asSequence()
                    .filter { group.isTrackSelected(it) }
                    .map { group.getTrackFormat(it) }
            }
            .toList()
        val videoBps = selectedFormats.firstOrNull { it.width > 0 && it.bitrate > 0 }?.bitrate
            ?: selectedFormats.sumOf { it.bitrate.coerceAtLeast(0) }
        when {
            videoBps >= 1_000_000 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f Mbps", videoBps / 1_000_000.0)
            videoBps > 0 -> "${videoBps / 1_000} kbps"
            else -> null
        }
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Rtl) {
        when (displayMode) {
            // =========================================================================
            // MODE A: PHONE PORTRAIT — Vertical stacked layout (16:9 video top + list below)
            // =========================================================================
            DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT -> {
                PhonePortraitPlayerScreen(
                    exo = exo,
                    item = item,
                    playlist = playlist,
                    currentIndex = index,
                    isLive = isLive,
                    canSeek = canSeek,
                    isPlaying = isPlaying,
                    buffering = buffering,
                    reconnecting = reconnecting,
                    error = error,
                    progressFlow = vm.progress,
                    playbackSpeed = playbackSpeed,
                    tracks = tracks,
                    textOff = textOff,
                    hasVideoOverride = hasVideoOverride,
                    currentProgram = currentProgram,
                    nextProgram = nextProgram,
                    seriesDetails = seriesDetails,
                    controlsVisible = controlsVisible,
                    resizeMode = ResizeModes[resizeIndex % ResizeModes.size],
                    onToggleControls = {
                        controlsVisible = !controlsVisible
                        interactions++
                    },
                    onBack = exitPlayerNow,
                    onTogglePlay = togglePlay,
                    onSeekBy = seekBy,
                    onSeekTo = { target ->
                        exo.seekTo(target.coerceAtLeast(0L))
                        vm.onSeekPosition(target)
                        interactions++
                    },
                    onPrev = zapPrevious,
                    onNext = zapNext,
                    onSelectPlaylistIndex = goTo,
                    onSelectEpisode = playEpisodeFromDrawer,
                    onCycleSpeed = cycleSpeed,
                    onCycleResize = {
                        vm.cycleResize(ResizeModes.size)
                        interactions++
                    },
                    onOpenTracks = { showTracks = true },
                    onOpenGuide = { showGuide = true },
                    onEnterLandscape = toggleOrientation,
                    onRetry = { vm.retry() },
                )
            }

            // =========================================================================
            // MODE B & C: PHONE LANDSCAPE vs TABLET / TV FULLSCREEN
            // =========================================================================
            else -> {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(OsdSurfaceLowest)
                        .focusRequester(rootFocus)
                        .focusable()
                        .onPreviewKeyEvent(onKey),
                ) {
                    AndroidView(
                        factory = { ctx ->
                            PlayerView(ctx).apply {
                                useController = false
                                setShowBuffering(PlayerView.SHOW_BUFFERING_NEVER)
                                layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                                )
                                player = exo
                            }
                        },
                        update = {
                            it.resizeMode = ResizeModes[resizeIndex % ResizeModes.size]
                            if (isPlayerReleased && it.player != null) {
                                it.player = null
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    )

                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .pointerInput(Unit) {
                                detectTapGestures(onTap = {
                                    if (showEpisodesDrawer) {
                                        showEpisodesDrawer = false
                                    } else if (showQuickChannelsDrawer) {
                                        showQuickChannelsDrawer = false
                                    } else {
                                        controlsVisible = !controlsVisible
                                    }
                                    interactions++
                                })
                            },
                    )

                    if ((buffering || reconnecting) && error == null) {
                        Column(
                            Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                        ) {
                            CircularProgressIndicator(color = OsdCyan, trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                            if (reconnecting) {
                                Text(
                                    Ar.reconnecting,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = OsdCyan,
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    AnimatedVisibility(
                        visible = controlsVisible && !isInPipMode && error == null && LocalThemeCustomization.current.isVisible("player_controls"),
                        enter = fadeIn(tween(220)),
                        exit = fadeOut(tween(180)),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.displayCutout),
                    ) {
                        UnifiedFullscreenPlayerOsd(
                            item = item,
                            isLive = isLive,
                            canSeek = canSeek,
                            playlistCount = if (isLive) playlist.size else (seriesDetails?.episodes?.size?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: playlist.size),
                            currentProgram = currentProgram,
                            isPlaying = isPlaying,
                            progressFlow = vm.progress,
                            playbackSpeed = playbackSpeed,
                            showDrawer = if (isLive) showQuickChannelsDrawer else showEpisodesDrawer,
                            activeBitrateText = activeBitrateText,
                            playFocus = playFocus,
                            onBack = exitPlayerNow,
                            onTogglePlay = togglePlay,
                            onSeekBy = seekBy,
                            onSeekTo = { target ->
                                exo.seekTo(target.coerceAtLeast(0L))
                                vm.onSeekPosition(target)
                                interactions++
                            },
                            onPrev = zapPrevious,
                            onNext = zapNext,
                            onCycleSpeed = cycleSpeed,
                            onCycleResize = {
                                vm.cycleResize(ResizeModes.size)
                                interactions++
                            },
                            onOpenTracks = {
                                showTracks = true
                                interactions++
                            },
                            onToggleDrawer = {
                                if (isLive) showQuickChannelsDrawer = !showQuickChannelsDrawer
                                else showEpisodesDrawer = !showEpisodesDrawer
                                interactions++
                            },
                        )
                    }

                    // Side Channels Drawer for Live TV (Phone Landscape & TV, aligned to right side in RTL)
                    if (isLive) {
                        AnimatedVisibility(
                            visible = showQuickChannelsDrawer && error == null,
                            enter = slideInHorizontally(tween(260)) { it } + fadeIn(tween(200)),
                            exit = slideOutHorizontally(tween(220)) { it } + fadeOut(tween(160)),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                                .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.displayCutout),
                        ) {
                            LiveChannelsDrawer(
                                playlist = playlist,
                                currentIndex = index,
                                currentProgram = currentProgram,
                                compact = displayMode == DisplayMode.PHONE_LANDSCAPE,
                                drawerFocus = drawerFocus,
                                onSelectChannel = { idx ->
                                    goTo(idx)
                                    showQuickChannelsDrawer = false
                                },
                                onClose = { showQuickChannelsDrawer = false },
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Side Episodes Drawer for VOD (aligned to right side in RTL)
                    if (!isLive) {
                        AnimatedVisibility(
                            visible = showEpisodesDrawer && error == null,
                            enter = slideInHorizontally(tween(260)) { it } + fadeIn(tween(200)),
                            exit = slideOutHorizontally(tween(220)) { it } + fadeOut(tween(160)),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                                .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.displayCutout),
                        ) {
                            VodEpisodesDrawer(
                                currentItem = item,
                                playlist = playlist,
                                currentIndex = index,
                                seriesDetails = seriesDetails,
                                progressFlow = vm.progress,
                                compact = displayMode == DisplayMode.PHONE_LANDSCAPE,
                                drawerFocus = drawerFocus,
                                onSelectPlaylistIndex = { idx ->
                                    goTo(idx)
                                    showEpisodesDrawer = false
                                },
                                onSelectEpisode = { ep ->
                                    playEpisodeFromDrawer(ep)
                                    showEpisodesDrawer = false
                                },
                                onClose = { showEpisodesDrawer = false },
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    error?.let { message ->
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.Center)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                                .background(OsdSurfaceLow.copy(alpha = 0.94f))
                                .border(1.dp, OsdOutlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.5f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                                .padding(28.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                message,
                                fontSize = 18.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = OsdOnSurface,
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                                GlassButton(
                                    Ar.retry,
                                    { vm.retry() },
                                    primary = true,
                                    autoFocus = true,
                                )
                                GlassButton(Ar.back, exitPlayerNow)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showTracks) {
        TracksDialog(tracks, exo, trackSelectionParams) { showTracks = false }
    }
    if (showGuide && isLive) {
        EpgDialog(channelName = item.title, channelId = item.id, onDismiss = { showGuide = false })
    }
}
