/**
 * يستخرج رمز استجابة HTTP الفعلي عبر تتبع سلسلة `cause` لـ [HttpDataSource.InvalidResponseCodeException]
 * لتفادي التقاط أرقام موجودة داخل نص الرابط مثل `/403.mp4`.
 */
fun extractHttpResponseCode(throwable: Throwable?): Int? {
    var cur: Throwable? = throwable
    var depth = 0
    while (cur != null && depth < 12) {
        if (cur is HttpDataSource.InvalidResponseCodeException) {
            return cur.responseCode
        }
        cur = cur.cause
        depth++
    }
    return null
}

data class PlaybackProgress(
    val position: Long = 0L,
    val bufferedPosition: Long = 0L,
    val duration: Long = 0L,
)

@Immutable
data class PlayerUiState(
    val playlist: List<PlayItem>,
    val index: Int,
    val isPlaying: Boolean = false,
    val buffering: Boolean = true,
    val error: String? = null,
    val playbackSpeed: Float = 1.0f,
    val speedIndex: Int = 0,
    val resizeIndex: Int = 0,
    val retryKey: Int = 0,
    val formatAttempt: Int = 0,
    val autoReconnectCount: Int = 0,
    val reconnecting: Boolean = false,
    val seriesDetails: SeriesDetails? = null,
    val requestPlayFocusTick: Int = 0,
) {
    val currentItem: PlayItem
        get() = playlist.getOrElse(index.coerceAtLeast(0)) { PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, "", "") }
    val isLive: Boolean
        get() = currentItem.kind == Kind.LIVE
}

class PlayerViewModel(
    private val container: AppContainer,
    private val initialRequest: PlayRequest,
) : ViewModel() {

    private val repo = container.repo

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(
        PlayerUiState(
            playlist = initialRequest.items,
            index = initialRequest.startIndex.coerceIn(0, initialRequest.items.lastIndex.coerceAtLeast(0)),
        ),
    )
    val state: StateFlow<PlayerUiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val _progress = MutableStateFlow(PlaybackProgress())
    val progress: StateFlow<PlaybackProgress> = _progress.asStateFlow()

    private var forceFromZeroId: String? = null
    private var usedInitialPos: Boolean = false
    private var lastLoadedSeriesGroupId: String? = null
    private var retryJob: Job? = null

    fun saveProgress(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        val current = _state.value.currentItem
        if (current.id.isBlank()) return
        container.saveProgress(current, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun saveProgressIfVod(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        val current = _state.value.currentItem
        if (current.id.isBlank() || current.kind == Kind.LIVE) return
        container.saveProgress(current, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun flushRecents() {
        container.flushRecents()
    }

    fun onPlaybackPausedOrStopped(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        container.flushRecents()
    }

    fun loadSeriesIfNeeded(item: PlayItem) {
        if (item.kind != Kind.EPISODE || item.groupId.isBlank()) return
        if (lastLoadedSeriesGroupId == item.groupId && _state.value.seriesDetails != null) return
        lastLoadedSeriesGroupId = item.groupId
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val loaded = runCatching { repo.seriesDetails(item.groupId) }.getOrNull() ?: return@launch
            _state.update { s ->
                val expanded = if (loaded.episodes.isNotEmpty() && s.playlist.size <= 1) {
                    loaded.episodes.map { e ->
                        PlayItem(
                            kind = Kind.EPISODE,
                            id = e.id,
                            title = loaded.name.ifBlank { item.title },
                            subtitle = "${Ar.seasonEpisode(e.season, e.number)}: ${e.title}",
                            icon = e.image.ifBlank { loaded.cover.ifBlank { item.icon } },
                            ext = e.ext,
                            groupId = loaded.id.ifBlank { item.groupId },
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    s.playlist
                }
                val matchIdx = expanded.indexOfFirst { it.id == s.currentItem.id }
                s.copy(
                    seriesDetails = loaded,
                    playlist = expanded,
                    index = if (matchIdx >= 0) matchIdx else s.index,
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun goTo(target: Int, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val current = _state.value
        if (target !in 0..current.playlist.lastIndex || target == current.index) return false
        retryJob?.cancel()
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                formatAttempt = 0,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                index = target,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    fun zapNext(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.playlist.size <= 1) return false
        val target = if (s.index >= s.playlist.lastIndex) 0 else s.index + 1
        return goTo(target, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun zapPrevious(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.playlist.size <= 1) return false
        val target = if (s.index <= 0) s.playlist.lastIndex else s.index - 1
        return goTo(target, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun playEpisodeFromDrawer(ep: Episode, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        val existingIdx = s.playlist.indexOfFirst { it.id == ep.id }
        if (existingIdx >= 0) {
            return goTo(existingIdx, positionMs, durationMs)
        }
        val details = s.seriesDetails ?: return false
        val allEps = details.episodes
        if (allEps.isEmpty()) return false
        val sName = details.name.ifBlank { s.currentItem.title }
        val sCover = details.cover.ifBlank { s.currentItem.icon }
        val sId = details.id.ifBlank { s.currentItem.groupId }
        val newItems = allEps.map { e ->
            PlayItem(
                kind = Kind.EPISODE,
                id = e.id,
                title = sName,
                subtitle = "${Ar.seasonEpisode(e.season, e.number)}: ${e.title}",
                icon = e.image.ifBlank { sCover },
                ext = e.ext,
                groupId = sId,
            )
        }
        val newIdx = newItems.indexOfFirst { it.id == ep.id }.coerceAtLeast(0)
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                playlist = newItems,
                formatAttempt = 0,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                index = newIdx,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    fun cycleSpeed(speeds: FloatArray): Float {
        val nextIdx = (_state.value.speedIndex + 1) % speeds.size
        val nextSpeed = speeds[nextIdx]
        _state.update { it.copy(speedIndex = nextIdx, playbackSpeed = nextSpeed) }
        return nextSpeed
    }

    fun cycleResize(modeCount: Int) {
        _state.update { it.copy(resizeIndex = (it.resizeIndex + 1) % modeCount) }
    }

    fun retry() {
        retryJob?.cancel()
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                formatAttempt = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                retryKey = it.retryKey + 1,
            )
        }
    }

    fun bumpPlayFocusTick() {
        _state.update { it.copy(requestPlayFocusTick = it.requestPlayFocusTick + 1) }
    }

    fun onIsPlayingChanged(playing: Boolean) {
        _state.update { it.copy(isPlaying = playing) }
    }

    fun onProgressUpdate(position: Long, buffered: Long, duration: Long) {
        _progress.value = PlaybackProgress(position, buffered, duration)
    }

    fun onSeekPosition(position: Long) {
        _progress.update { it.copy(position = position) }
    }

    fun onBufferingReady(isBuffering: Boolean, isReady: Boolean) {
        _state.update { s ->
            if (isReady) {
                s.copy(
                    buffering = isBuffering,
                    error = null,
                    reconnecting = false,
                    autoReconnectCount = 0,
                )
            } else {
                s.copy(buffering = isBuffering)
            }
        }
    }

    fun onPrepareStarted() {
        _state.update { it.copy(error = null, buffering = true) }
    }

    fun onUrlError() {
        _state.update { it.copy(reconnecting = false, error = Ar.errPlayback) }
    }

    fun onPlayerError(errorCodeName: String, responseCode: Int? = null) {
        retryJob?.cancel()

        val isDeviceConflict = responseCode == 401 || responseCode == 403 || responseCode == 458
        val s = _state.value
        val live = s.isLive

        // Device-conflict: server keeps the old session alive for several
        // seconds after ExoPlayer released it. Immediate retry => another 403.
        if (isDeviceConflict) {
            if (s.autoReconnectCount < 2) {
                val waitMs = 5000L * (s.autoReconnectCount + 1)
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "تعارض اتصال — إعادة المحاولة بعد ${waitMs / 1000} ثانية...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(waitMs)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = it.autoReconnectCount + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
                return
            }
            if (live && s.formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size) {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ تبديل الصيغة بعد 5 ثوانٍ...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(5000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = 0,
                        formatAttempt = it.formatAttempt + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
                return
            }
            _state.update { it.copy(
                reconnecting = false,
                error = "تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للأجهزة المتصلة أو انتهى الاشتراك ($responseCode)",
            ) }
            return
        }

        // Network / other errors — short backoff
        when {
            s.autoReconnectCount < 2 -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ إعادة الاتصال بعد 2 ثانية...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(2000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = it.autoReconnectCount + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
            }
            live && s.formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ تبديل الصيغة بعد 3 ثوانٍ...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(3000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = 0,
                        formatAttempt = it.formatAttempt + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
            }
            else -> {
                val hint = when (responseCode) {
                    404 -> "القناة أو المحتوى غير متوفر حالياً على السيرفر (404)"
                    500, 502, 503 -> "خلل في خادم البث ($responseCode)"
                    else -> if (errorCodeName.isNotBlank()) "فشل تشغيل البث ($errorCodeName)" else Ar.errPlayback
                }
                _state.update { it.copy(reconnecting = false, error = hint) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun streamUrl(item: PlayItem, formatAttempt: Int): String = repo.streamUrl(item, formatAttempt)

    /**
     * LIVE-01: called by [LiveVideoStallWatchdog] after a repeated silent video-decoder stall.
     * Switches to the next live container (m3u8 <-> ts) if one is available.
     * Returns true when a switch was scheduled (the prepare effect will reload the stream).
     */
    fun switchLiveFormatAfterStall(): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (!s.isLive) return false
        val formats = repo.liveExtensions().size
        if (formats < 2) return false
        retryJob?.cancel()
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                formatAttempt = (it.formatAttempt + 1) % formats,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    suspend fun resolveResumePositionMs(item: PlayItem, currentPlayerPositionMs: Long): Long {
        val s = _state.value
        val resume = when {
            item.kind == Kind.LIVE || initialRequest.fromStart || forceFromZeroId == item.id -> 0L
            s.autoReconnectCount > 0 && currentPlayerPositionMs > 0L -> currentPlayerPositionMs
            initialRequest.initialPositionMs > 0L && !usedInitialPos &&
                item.id == initialRequest.items.getOrNull(initialRequest.startIndex)?.id -> {
                usedInitialPos = true
                initialRequest.initialPositionMs
            }
            else -> container.prefs.positionOf(item.kind, item.id)
        }
        if (forceFromZeroId == item.id) forceFromZeroId = null
        return resume.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    }

    fun onPlaybackEnded(totalDurationMs: Long, onNoNextItem: () -> Unit) {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.isLive) return
        if (totalDurationMs > 0L) {
            container.saveProgress(s.currentItem, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
        }
        val nextInPlaylist = if (s.index < s.playlist.lastIndex) s.playlist[s.index + 1] else null
        if (nextInPlaylist != null) {
            forceFromZeroId = nextInPlaylist.id
            goTo(s.index + 1, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
            return
        }
        val allEps = s.seriesDetails?.episodes.orEmpty()
        val curEpIdx = allEps.indexOfFirst { it.id == s.currentItem.id }
        val nextEp = if (curEpIdx >= 0 && curEpIdx + 1 < allEps.size) allEps[curEpIdx + 1] else null
        if (nextEp != null) {
            forceFromZeroId = nextEp.id
            playEpisodeFromDrawer(nextEp, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
        } else {
            onNoNextItem()
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/SahabPlaybackService.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import androidx.annotation.OptIn
import androidx.media3.common.Player
import androidx.media3.common.util.UnstableApi
import androidx.media3.session.MediaSession
import androidx.media3.session.MediaSessionService
import com.saydlawy.sahab.MainActivity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow

/**
 * O-03: Coordinates the active [Player] with [SahabPlaybackService] (background live audio)
 * and phone Picture-in-Picture state without affecting Android TV behavior.
 */
object SahabMediaSessionBridge {
    @Volatile
    var activePlayer: Player? = null
        private set

    @Volatile
    var pipAllowed: Boolean = false

    val inPipMode = MutableStateFlow(false)

    fun attach(context: Context, player: Player, allowBackgroundService: Boolean) {
        activePlayer = player
        if (allowBackgroundService) {
            runCatching {
                context.applicationContext.startService(
                    Intent(context.applicationContext, SahabPlaybackService::class.java),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun detach(context: Context, player: Player) {
        if (activePlayer === player) {
            activePlayer = null
            pipAllowed = false
            inPipMode.value = false
            runCatching {
                context.applicationContext.stopService(
                    Intent(context.applicationContext, SahabPlaybackService::class.java),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
class SahabPlaybackService : MediaSessionService() {

    private var mediaSession: MediaSession? = null

    private fun buildSession(player: Player): MediaSession {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
        }
        val sessionActivity = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        return MediaSession.Builder(this, player)
            .setSessionActivity(sessionActivity)
            .build()
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        syncActiveSession()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        syncActiveSession()
        return super.onStartCommand(intent, flags, startId)
    }

    private fun syncActiveSession() {
        val player = SahabMediaSessionBridge.activePlayer ?: return
        val existing = mediaSession
        if (existing == null) {
            mediaSession = buildSession(player)
        } else if (existing.player !== player) {
            existing.player = player
        }
    }

    override fun onGetSession(controllerInfo: MediaSession.ControllerInfo): MediaSession? {
        if (!isAllowedMediaController(controllerInfo.uid, android.os.Process.myUid(), controllerInfo.isTrusted)) {
            return null
        }
        syncActiveSession()
        return mediaSession
    }

    internal companion object {
        fun isAllowedMediaController(uid: Int, myUid: Int, isTrusted: Boolean): Boolean =
            uid == myUid || isTrusted
    }

    override fun onTaskRemoved(rootIntent: Intent?) {
        val player = mediaSession?.player
        if (player == null || !player.playWhenReady) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        mediaSession?.release()
        mediaSession = null
        super.onDestroy()
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/SahabPlayer.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.OptIn
import androidx.media3.common.AudioAttributes
import androidx.media3.common.C
import androidx.media3.common.Format
import androidx.media3.common.MimeTypes
import androidx.media3.common.Tracks
import androidx.media3.common.util.UnstableApi
import androidx.media3.database.StandaloneDatabaseProvider
import androidx.media3.datasource.DataSource
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.CacheDataSource
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.CacheKeyFactory
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.LeastRecentlyUsedCacheEvictor
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.SimpleCache
import androidx.media3.datasource.okhttp.OkHttpDataSource
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.DefaultLoadControl
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.DefaultRenderersFactory
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.source.DefaultMediaSourceFactory
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.trackselection.DefaultTrackSelector
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.upstream.DefaultLoadErrorHandlingPolicy
import androidx.media3.extractor.DefaultExtractorsFactory
import androidx.media3.extractor.ts.DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import java.io.File
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/**
 * Shared Media3 (ExoPlayer) factory with:
 * - Singleton 250 MB [SimpleCache] on disk for VOD (Movies & Series episodes) so scrubbing/rewinding/reopening is instant.
 * - Ultra-fast startup buffer thresholds ([DefaultLoadControl]) for sub-second playback start.
 * - Hardware/Software decoder fallback & TS/HLS/DASH/RTSP support.
 */
@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
object SahabPlayer {

    const val SEEK_STEP_MS = 10_000L
    private const val VIDEO_JOINING_TIME_MS = 3_000L
    private const val MEDIA_CACHE_BYTES = 512L * 1024L * 1024L // 512 MB

    @Volatile
    private var simpleCache: SimpleCache? = null

    @Synchronized
    private fun getMediaCache(context: Context): SimpleCache? {
        simpleCache?.let { return it }
        return runCatching {
            val appContext = context.applicationContext
            val dir = File(appContext.cacheDir, MEDIA_CACHE_DIR).apply { mkdirs() }
            val evictor = LeastRecentlyUsedCacheEvictor(MEDIA_CACHE_BYTES)
            val dbProvider = StandaloneDatabaseProvider(appContext)
            SimpleCache(dir, evictor, dbProvider).also { simpleCache = it }
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun clearMediaCache(context: Context) {
        runCatching { simpleCache?.release() }
        simpleCache = null
        runCatching {
            File(context.applicationContext.cacheDir, MEDIA_CACHE_DIR).deleteRecursively()
        }
    }

    private const val MEDIA_CACHE_DIR = "sahab_media_cache"
    private val CACHE_KEY_CREDENTIAL_REGEX = Regex("/(live|movie|series)/[^/]+/[^/]+/")

    internal val leaseCoordinator = PlayerLeaseCoordinator()
    private val playerHandles = java.util.Collections.synchronizedMap(
        java.util.IdentityHashMap<ExoPlayer, ExoPlayerLeaseHandle>(),
    )

    private class ExoPlayerLeaseHandle(
        private val player: ExoPlayer,
        override val role: PlayerRole,
    ) : LeasedPlayerHandle {
        @Volatile
        var onRevoked: (() -> Unit)? = null

        override fun stopAndRelease() {
            playerHandles.remove(player)
            onRevoked?.invoke()
            onRevoked = null
            runCatching { player.stop() }
            runCatching { player.clearMediaItems() }
            runCatching { player.release() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Binds an optional callback invoked when [player] is released or preempted by another player lease.
     */
    fun bindOnRelease(player: ExoPlayer, onReleased: () -> Unit) {
        playerHandles[player]?.onRevoked = onReleased
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops, clears, and releases [player] once via [leaseCoordinator].
     */
    fun releasePlayer(player: ExoPlayer?) {
        if (player == null) return
        val handle = playerHandles.remove(player)
        if (handle != null) {
            leaseCoordinator.release(handle)
        } else {
            runCatching { player.stop() }
            runCatching { player.clearMediaItems() }
            runCatching { player.release() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops and releases any active preview player before fullscreen playback begins.
     */
    fun releasePreviewPlayers() {
        leaseCoordinator.releaseByRole(PlayerRole.PREVIEW)
    }

    fun warmUpMediaCache(context: Context) {
        getMediaCache(context)
    }

    fun create(
        context: Context,
        client: OkHttpClient,
        isLive: Boolean,
        isPreview: Boolean = false,
        accountHash: String = "anon",
    ): ExoPlayer {
        // Release any existing active player lease before allocating a new ExoPlayer/MediaCodec instance.
        leaseCoordinator.releaseAll()
        val renderers = DefaultRenderersFactory(context)
            .setEnableDecoderFallback(true)
            .setExtensionRendererMode(DefaultRenderersFactory.EXTENSION_RENDERER_MODE_PREFER)
            // LIVE-01: let video join the already-running audio clock without dropping the first frames.
            .setAllowedVideoJoiningTimeMs(VIDEO_JOINING_TIME_MS)

        // LIVE-01: adapt across any quality / fps / codec / audio format the provider sends.
        val trackSelector = DefaultTrackSelector(
            context,
            DefaultTrackSelector.Parameters.Builder(context)
                .setAllowVideoMixedMimeTypeAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowVideoNonSeamlessAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedMimeTypeAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedSampleRateAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedChannelCountAdaptiveness(true)
                .setExceedVideoConstraintsIfNecessary(true)
                .setExceedRendererCapabilitiesIfNecessary(true)
                .build(),
        )

        val extractors = DefaultExtractorsFactory()
            .setConstantBitrateSeekingEnabled(true)
            .setTsExtractorFlags(
                DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory.FLAG_ALLOW_NON_IDR_KEYFRAMES or
                    DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory.FLAG_DETECT_ACCESS_UNITS,
            )

        // LIVE-01: live segments/playlists must never go through the shared 50 MB OkHttp disk cache
        // (stale .m3u8 + disk write latency). newBuilder() keeps the shared connection pool/dispatcher.
        val httpClient = if (isLive) client.newBuilder().cache(null).build() else client
        val httpFactory = OkHttpDataSource.Factory(httpClient)
            .setUserAgent("Sahab/1.0 (Android)")

        val dataSourceFactory: DataSource.Factory = if (!isLive) {
            val cache = getMediaCache(context)
            if (cache != null) {
                val sanitizedKeyFactory = CacheKeyFactory { dataSpec ->
                    val rawKey = dataSpec.key ?: dataSpec.uri.toString()
                    // Isolate cache per account: /kind/<accountHash>/<id>.<ext>
                    // (hash derived from server+user; never stores credentials in cache keys)
                    rawKey.replace(CACHE_KEY_CREDENTIAL_REGEX, "/$1/$accountHash/")
                }
                CacheDataSource.Factory()
                    .setCache(cache)
                    .setCacheKeyFactory(sanitizedKeyFactory)
                    .setUpstreamDataSourceFactory(httpFactory)
                    .setFlags(CacheDataSource.FLAG_IGNORE_CACHE_ON_ERROR)
            } else {
                httpFactory
            }
        } else {
            httpFactory
        }

        val sources = DefaultMediaSourceFactory(context, extractors)
            .setDataSourceFactory(dataSourceFactory)
            .setLoadErrorHandlingPolicy(DefaultLoadErrorHandlingPolicy(if (isLive) 4 else 3))
            .apply {
                if (isLive) {
                    // LIVE-01: never speed up / slow down live playback to chase the live edge.
                    // Speed changes skew the AudioTrack clock and trigger video drop-frame storms.
                    setLiveMinSpeed(1f)
                    setLiveMaxSpeed(1f)
                }
            }

        val loadControl = when {
            isPreview -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 4_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 12_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 450,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 1_000,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .build()
            isLive -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 10_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 30_000,
                    // LIVE-01: fast start but with enough reorder headroom for 50/60fps HEVC/FHD/4K.
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 1_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 2_500,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .setBackBuffer(0, false)
                .build()
            else -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 20_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 60_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 800,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 1_800,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .build()
        }

        return ExoPlayer.Builder(context, renderers)
            .setTrackSelector(trackSelector)
            .setMediaSourceFactory(sources)
            .setLoadControl(loadControl)
            .setAudioAttributes(AudioAttributes.DEFAULT, !isPreview)
            .setHandleAudioBecomingNoisy(!isPreview)
            .setWakeMode(C.WAKE_MODE_NETWORK)
            .setSeekBackIncrementMs(SEEK_STEP_MS)
            .setSeekForwardIncrementMs(SEEK_STEP_MS)
            .build()
            .also { created ->
                val role = if (isPreview) PlayerRole.PREVIEW else PlayerRole.FULLSCREEN
                val handle = ExoPlayerLeaseHandle(created, role)
                playerHandles[created] = handle
                leaseCoordinator.acquire(handle)
            }
    }

    /** Formats a live technical video badge from active tracks. */
    fun videoBadge(tracks: Tracks, fallbackQuality: String? = null): String {
        val selectedFormat = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { group ->
                (0 until group.length).firstOrNull { group.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { group.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (group.length > 0) group.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            }

        if (selectedFormat != null && selectedFormat.height > 0) {
            val h = selectedFormat.height
            val res = when {
                h >= 2160 -> "4K"
                h >= 1440 -> "2K"
                h >= 1080 -> "1080p"
                h >= 720 -> "720p"
                h >= 540 -> "540p"
                else -> "${h}p"
            }
            val fps = if (selectedFormat.frameRate > 0f) {
                " ${selectedFormat.frameRate.roundToInt()}fps"
            } else ""
            return "$res$fps"
        }
        return fallbackQuality?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "HD"
    }

    /** Formats an audio stream badge from active tracks. */
    fun audioBadge(tracks: Tracks): String? {
        val selectedFormat = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { group ->
                (0 until group.length).firstOrNull { group.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { group.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (group.length > 0) group.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            } ?: return null

        val langCode = selectedFormat.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            selectedFormat.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "الصوت"
        }

        val codec = codecShortName(selectedFormat)
        return if (codec != null) "$langName ($codec)" else langName
    }

    fun videoQualityLabel(format: Format): String {
        val h = format.height
        val res = when {
            h >= 2160 -> "4K (${h}p)"
            h >= 1080 -> "FHD (${h}p)"
            h >= 720 -> "HD (${h}p)"
            h > 0 -> "SD (${h}p)"
            else -> format.label ?: "مسار فيديو"
        }
        val fps = if (format.frameRate > 0f) " · ${format.frameRate.roundToInt()}fps" else ""
        val bitrate = if (format.bitrate > 0) " · ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", format.bitrate / 1_000_000f)} Mbps" else ""
        return "$res$fps$bitrate"
    }

    fun activeBitrateText(tracks: Tracks, isLive: Boolean): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
        if (format != null && format.bitrate > 0) {
            return String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f Mbps", format.bitrate / 1_000_000f)
        }
        return ""
    }

    fun activeAudioLabel(tracks: Tracks): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g ->
                (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (g.length > 0) g.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            } ?: return "الصوت الافتراضي"

        val langCode = format.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            format.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "الصوت"
        }
        val codec = codecShortName(format)
        return if (codec != null) "$langName ($codec)" else langName
    }

    fun activeSubtitleLabel(tracks: Tracks, disabled: Boolean): String {
        if (disabled) return "بدون ترجمة"
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
            ?: return "بدون ترجمة"
        val langCode = format.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            format.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "ترجمة"
        }
        return "$langName (CC)"
    }

    fun activeQualityButtonLabel(tracks: Tracks, hasOverride: Boolean, isLive: Boolean): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
        val h = format?.height ?: 0
        val res = when {
            h >= 2160 -> "4K"
            h >= 1080 -> "1080p FHD"
            h >= 720 -> "720p HD"
            h > 0 -> "${h}p"
            else -> ""
        }
        return when {
            res.isBlank() -> if (hasOverride) "الجودة" else "تلقائي"
            hasOverride -> res
            else -> "تلقائي $res"
        }
    }

    private fun codecShortName(format: Format): String? {
        val mime = format.sampleMimeType ?: return null
        return when (mime) {
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_AAC -> "AAC"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_AC3 -> "AC3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_E_AC3, MimeTypes.AUDIO_E_AC3_JOC -> "EAC3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_MPEG -> "MP3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_OPUS -> "OPUS"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_VORBIS -> "Vorbis"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_FLAC -> "FLAC"
            else -> mime.substringAfter('/', "").uppercase(Locale.US).takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun CloudAccountPanel(
    email: String?,
    onLogin: () -> Unit,
    onLogout: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var currentPinConfigured by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pinInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var savingPin by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(email) {
        currentPinConfigured = !container.prefs.getCloudSyncPin().isNullOrEmpty()
    }

    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .size(44.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .background(Palette.Blue.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                            .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    ) {
                        Icon(Ic.Cloud, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text("الحساب السحابي", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                        Text(
                            if (email != null) "متصل" else "غير متصل",
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            color = if (email != null) Palette.Emerald else Palette.Gold,
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (email != null) Tag("Cloud", Palette.Blue)
            }
            if (email != null) {
                InfoRow("البريد الإلكتروني", email)
                InfoRow(
                    "تشفير الاشتراكات (Sync PIN)",
                    if (currentPinConfigured) "مفعّل (PBKDF2-GCM)" else "محلي فقط (Android Keystore)",
                )
                Text(
                    text = if (currentPinConfigured) {
                        "تُشفَّر بيانات الاشتراك على السحابة برمز المزامنة الخاص بك؛ أدخله عند الاستعادة على جهاز جديد."
                    } else {
                        "بدون رمز مزامنة (Sync PIN)، تبقى بيانات الاشتراك مشفرة محلياً عبر Android Keystore ولا تُرفع للسحابة."
                    },
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                )
                GlassTextField(
                    value = pinInput,
                    onValueChange = { pinInput = it },
                    placeholder = "رمز حماية المزامنة (4+ أحرف أو أرقام)",
                    keyboardType = androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType.Password,
                    password = true,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = if (savingPin) "جاري الحفظ..." else "حفظ الرمز ومزامنة",
                        primary = true,
                        onClick = {
                            if (savingPin) return@GlassButton
                            val trimmed = pinInput.trim()
                            if (trimmed.length < 4) {
                                Toast.makeText(context, "أدخل 4 أحرف/أرقام على الأقل", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                return@GlassButton
                            }
                            savingPin = true
                            scope.launch {
                                container.prefs.setCloudSyncPin(trimmed)
                                val accounts = container.prefs.getAccounts()
                                if (accounts.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(accounts, pinOverride = trimmed)
                                }
                                currentPinConfigured = true
                                pinInput = ""
                                savingPin = false
                                Toast.makeText(context, "تم تفعيل تشفير المزامنة برمز الحماية", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    if (currentPinConfigured) {
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إلغاء الرمز",
                            onClick = {
                                scope.launch {
                                    container.prefs.setCloudSyncPin(null)
                                    currentPinConfigured = false
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "تم التحويل للتخزين المحلي فقط (Keystore)", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تسجيل الخروج من السحابة",
                    onClick = onLogout,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cloud_logout_btn"),
                    icon = Ic.Close,
                )
            } else {
                Text("لم يتم تسجيل الدخول، بياناتك تحفظ محلياً على الجهاز.", fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تسجيل الدخول السحابي",
                    onClick = onLogin,
                    primary = true,
                    icon = Ic.Cloud,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cloud_login_btn"),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun AccountPanel(
    account: Account?,
    info: UserInfo?,
    onSyncNow: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Person, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text("اشتراك المشاهدة (Xtream)", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text(account?.user.orEmpty().ifBlank { "-" }, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.Glow)
                }
            }
            val activeStatus = info?.status.orEmpty().ifBlank { "Active" }
            Tag(activeStatus, Palette.Emerald)
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
        InfoRow("الخادم", hostOf(account?.server.orEmpty()))
        InfoRow("اسم المستخدم", account?.user.orEmpty())
        if (info != null) {
            InfoRow("الحالة", if (info.status.isBlank()) "Active" else info.status)
            val expiry = info.expiry
            InfoRow("ينتهي الاشتراك", if (expiry == null || expiry <= 0L) Ar.subscriptionOpenShort else formatDate(expiry))
            if (info.maxConnections.isNotBlank()) {
                InfoRow("الأجهزة المتصلة", "${info.activeConnections.ifBlank { "0" }} / ${info.maxConnections}")
            }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
        GlassButton(
            text = "تحديث ومزامنة قوائم القنوات والأفلام الآن",
            onClick = onSyncNow,
            icon = Ic.Refresh,
            primary = true,
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_sync_now_btn"),
        )
    }
}

@Composable

            itemKey = "settings_toggle_light_mode",

}

@Composable
internal fun AiKeysPanel(aiKeys: ApiKeyManager, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    val keys by aiKeys.keys.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())
    val activeId by aiKeys.activeId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = "")
    var showAdd by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var newKeyText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var newKeyLabel by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            DotTitle("مفاتيح Gemini", Palette.Glow, fontSize = 16.sp)
            Text("أضف مفاتيح لتوليد قصص الأفلام باللغة العربية عند الحاجة.", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
            if (!aiKeys.isEncrypted) {
                Text(
                    "تنبيه: جهازك لا يدعم التشفير الآمن للمفاتيح، تم الاحتفاظ بها في الذاكرة المؤقتة فقط ولن تُحفظ بنص صريح على القرص.",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.Gold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.12f))
                        .padding(10.dp),
                )
            }
            keys.forEach { key ->
                Row(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(if (key.id == activeId) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.04f))
                        .padding(12.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(key.label, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                        Text(key.value.take(8) + "…" + key.value.takeLast(4), fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                    }
                    if (key.id == activeId) {
                        Tag("نشط", Palette.Emerald)
                    } else {
                        GlassButton(text = "تفعيل", onClick = { aiKeys.setActive(key.id) })
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    GlassButton(text = "حذف", onClick = { aiKeys.removeKey(key.id) })
                }
            }
            GlassButton(
                text = "+ إضافة مفتاح",
                onClick = {
                    focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                    showAdd = true
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn"),
                primary = true,
            )
        }
    }

    if (showAdd) {
        Dialog(
            onDismissRequest = {
                showAdd = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
            },
        ) {
            GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 22.dp) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .imePadding(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                ) {
                    Text("مفتاح Gemini جديد", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    GlassTextField(value = newKeyLabel, onValueChange = { newKeyLabel = it }, placeholder = "اسم مختصر (اختياري)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    GlassTextField(value = newKeyText, onValueChange = { newKeyText = it }, placeholder = "المفتاح (AIza...)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إضافة",
                            onClick = {
                                if (aiKeys.addKey(newKeyText, newKeyLabel) != null) {
                                    newKeyText = ""
                                    newKeyLabel = ""
                                    showAdd = false
                                    focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                                }
                            },
                            primary = true,
                        )
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إلغاء",
                            onClick = {
                                showAdd = false
                                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.settings

import androidx.compose.foundation.background

private data class ThemeOption(val id: String, val title: String, val subtitle: String)

private val themeOptions = listOf(
    ThemeOption("cosmic_glass", "سحاب الكوني (الافتراضي)", "خلفيات كونية زجاجية متوازنة"),
    ThemeOption("cosmic_glass_pro", "Cosmic Glass Pro", "زجاج معزز وحدود أكثر وضوحاً"),

@Composable
internal fun SettingsAppearancePanel(container: AppContainer) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val prefs = container.prefs
    val saved by prefs.themePreferences.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = ThemePreferences())
    val moviesLayout by prefs.moviesLayoutStyle.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC)
    val seriesLayout by prefs.seriesLayoutStyle.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC)
    val light by prefs.lightEffects.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = false)

    val showRecents by prefs.showRecents.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showLiveChannels by prefs.showLiveChannelsOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showRecommended by prefs.showRecommendedOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showQuickCategories by prefs.showQuickCategoriesOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val homeChannelsLayout by prefs.homeChannelsLayout.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL)

    val activeThemeId = saved.selectedThemeId
    val customization = saved.customization(activeThemeId)

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
        VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
            title = "تخطيط شاشة الأفلام",
            subtitle = "اختر التصميم الخاص بقسم الأفلام بشكل مستقل",
            selected = moviesLayout,
            keyPrefix = "movies_layout",
            onSelect = { style -> scope.launch { prefs.setMoviesLayoutStyle(style) } },
        )

        VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
            title = "تخطيط شاشة المسلسلات",
            subtitle = "اختر التصميم الخاص بقسم المسلسلات بشكل مستقل",
            selected = seriesLayout,
            keyPrefix = "series_layout",
            onSelect = { style -> scope.launch { prefs.setSeriesLayoutStyle(style) } },
        )

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("ثيم التطبيق", Palette.Glow, fontSize = 18.sp)
                themeOptions.forEach { opt ->
                    val isSelected = activeThemeId == opt.id
                    GlassCard(
                        onClick = { scope.launch { prefs.setSelectedTheme(opt.id) } },
                        selected = isSelected,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_theme_${opt.id}"),
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        ) {
                            Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
                                Text(opt.title, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                Text(opt.subtitle, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                            if (isSelected) Tag("مطبق", Palette.Emerald)
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (activeThemeId == "oled_cinema") {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    DotTitle("لون التركيز في سينما OLED", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 15.sp)
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .focusGroup(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                    ) {
                        OledAccentPresets.forEach { preset ->
                            val selectedAccent = saved.oledAccentPreset == preset.id
                            GlassCard(
                                onClick = { scope.launch { prefs.setOledAccentPreset(preset.id) } },
                                selected = selectedAccent,
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_oled_accent_${preset.id}"),
                            ) {
                                Column(
                                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 10.dp, horizontal = 4.dp),
                                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        Modifier
                                            .size(22.dp)
                                            .clip(CircleShape)
                                            .background(preset.accent)
                                            .border(1.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.5f), CircleShape),
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = preset.nameAr,
                                        color = if (selectedAccent) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("الخط والزجاج", Palette.Royal, fontSize = 17.sp)
                AppearanceChoiceRow(
                    title = "نوع الخط",
                    options = listOf("cairo" to "القاهرة", "sans" to "بسيط", "serif" to "نسخي", "mono" to "أحادي"),
                    selected = customization.fontFamily,
                ) { font ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(fontFamily = font) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "حجم الخط",
                    value = customization.fontScale,
                    range = 0.8f..1.35f,
                    label = "${(customization.fontScale * 100).toInt()}٪",
                ) { scale ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(fontScale = scale) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "استدارة البطاقات",
                    value = customization.cornerRadius,
                    range = 0f..36f,
                    label = "${customization.cornerRadius.toInt()} dp",
                ) { radius ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(cornerRadius = radius) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "شفافية الزجاج",
                    value = customization.glassOpacity,
                    range = 0.2f..1f,
                    label = "${(customization.glassOpacity * 100).toInt()}٪",
                ) { opacity ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(glassOpacity = opacity) } }
                }
                var resetFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                Text(
                    text = "إعادة ضبط إعدادات هذا الثيم",
                    color = if (resetFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.Glow,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .onFocusChanged { resetFocused = it.isFocused }
                        .then(
                            if (resetFocused) Modifier.border(1.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan, RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                            else Modifier,
                        )
                        .clickable { scope.launch { prefs.resetThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) } }
                        .padding(vertical = 8.dp, horizontal = 10.dp),
                )
            }
        }

        AppearancePanel(
            light = light,
            onToggleLight = { scope.launch { prefs.setLightEffects(!light) } },
        )

        HomeCustomizationPanel(
            showRecents = showRecents,
            showLiveChannels = showLiveChannels,
            showRecommended = showRecommended,
            showQuickCategories = showQuickCategories,
            homeChannelsLayout = homeChannelsLayout,
            onToggleShowRecents = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowRecents(!showRecents) } },
            onToggleShowLive = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowLiveChannelsOnHome(!showLiveChannels) } },
            onToggleShowRecommended = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowRecommendedOnHome(!showRecommended) } },
            onToggleShowQuickCategories = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowQuickCategoriesOnHome(!showQuickCategories) } },
            onSelectLayout = { layout -> scope.launch { prefs.setHomeChannelsLayout(layout) } },
            onClearRecents = {
                scope.launch {
                    prefs.clearRecentsForAccount(prefs.currentActiveAccountId())
                }
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: VodLayoutStyle,
    keyPrefix: String,
    onSelect: (VodLayoutStyle) -> Unit,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            DotTitle(title, Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 18.sp)
            Text(subtitle, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 12.sp)
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                VodLayoutStyle.entries.forEach { style ->
                    val active = selected == style
                    GlassCard(
                        onClick = { onSelect(style) },
                        selected = active,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "${keyPrefix}_${style.id}"),
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        ) {
                            LayoutWireframePreview(style, active)
                            Row(
                                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            ) {
                                Text(style.labelAr, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold)
                                if (active) Tag("نشط", Palette.Emerald)
                            }
                            Text(style.descAr, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 11.sp, maxLines = 2)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LayoutWireframePreview(style: VodLayoutStyle, active: Boolean) {
    val barColor = if (active) Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f)
    val cardColor = if (active) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.45f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f)
    Column(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(48.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
            .padding(6.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp),
    ) {
        when (style) {
            VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC -> {
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp), modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Box(Modifier.width(18.dp).height(34.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                    Row(Modifier.weight(1f), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                        repeat(3) {
                            Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(34.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(cardColor))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            VodLayoutStyle.PRIME -> {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(16.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                    repeat(4) {
                        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(14.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(cardColor))
                    }
                }
            }
            VodLayoutStyle.NETFLIX -> {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(18.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                    repeat(5) {
                        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(cardColor))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppearanceChoiceRow(
    title: String,
    options: List<Pair<String, String>>,
    selected: String,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
        Text(title, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 13.sp)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.focusGroup(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        ) {
            options.forEach { (key, label) ->
                val active = selected == key
                var chipFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                Text(
                    text = label,
                    color = if (active || chipFocused) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .background(
                            when {
                                active -> Palette.Royal
                                chipFocused -> Palette.CardSurfaceElevated
                                else -> Palette.CardSurface
                            },
                        )
                        .onFocusChanged { chipFocused = it.isFocused }
                        .border(
                            width = if (chipFocused) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                            color = if (chipFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Color.Transparent,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        )
                        .clickable { onSelect(key) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppearanceSlider(
    title: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    label: String,
    onValue: (Float) -> Unit,
) {
    var localValue by remember(value) { mutableStateOf(value) }
    var sliderFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val step = ((range.endInclusive - range.start) / 20f).coerceAtLeast(0.02f)
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .onFocusChanged { sliderFocused = it.isFocused }
            .border(
                width = if (sliderFocused) 1.5.dp else 0.dp,
                color = if (sliderFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Color.Transparent,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
            )
            .padding(horizontal = if (sliderFocused) 8.dp else 0.dp, vertical = if (sliderFocused) 4.dp else 0.dp),
    ) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween) {
            Text(
                title,
                color = if (sliderFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.TextDim,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
            )
            Text(label, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
        Slider(
            value = localValue.coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive),
            onValueChange = { localValue = it },
            onValueChangeFinished = { onValue(localValue) },
            valueRange = range,
            modifier = Modifier.onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                if (event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown) {
                    when (event.nativeKeyEvent.keyCode) {
                        android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_RIGHT -> {
                            val next = (localValue + step).coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive)
                            localValue = next
                            onValue(next)
                            true
                        }
                        android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_LEFT -> {
                            val next = (localValue - step).coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive)
                            localValue = next
                            onValue(next)
                            true
                        }
                        else -> false
                    }
                } else {
                    false
                }
            },
        )
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.settings

import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.runtime.rememberCoroutineScope
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.saydlawy.sahab.AppContainer
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.HomeChannelsSource
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.DotTitle
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.GlassButton
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.GlassPanel
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@Composable
internal fun SettingsCustomTvPanel(
    container: AppContainer,
    homeChannelsSource: HomeChannelsSource,
    homeChannelsCategoryId: String,
    homeCustomChannelIds: Set<String>,
) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val dao = container.channelDb.channelDao()
    val activeAccountId by container.prefs.activeAccountId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(
        initialValue = container.prefs.currentActiveAccountId(),
    )
    val customLists by remember(dao, activeAccountId) {
        dao.observeLists(activeAccountId)
    }.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())
    val hiddenIds by dao.observeHiddenIds().collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
        HomeChannelsSelectionPanel(
            container = container,
            source = homeChannelsSource,
            selectedCategoryId = homeChannelsCategoryId,
            customChannelIds = homeCustomChannelIds,
            onSelectSource = { src -> scope.launch { container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(src) } },
            onSelectCategory = { catId ->
                scope.launch {
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsCategoryId(catId)
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY)
                }
            },
            onToggleCustomChannel = { chId ->
                scope.launch {
                    container.prefs.toggleHomeCustomChannel(chId)
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                }
            },
            onClearCustomChannels = {
                scope.launch { container.prefs.setHomeCustomChannelIds(emptySet()) }
            },
        )

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("قوائم البث المخصصة والقنوات المخفية", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 18.sp)
                Text(
                    text = "يمكنك إنشاء وتعديل القوائم وترتيب القنوات وإخفائها مباشرة من شاشة البث المباشر.",
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                )

                InfoRow("عدد القوائم المخصصة", "${customLists.size}")
                InfoRow("عدد القنوات المخفية", "${hiddenIds.size}")

                if (customLists.isNotEmpty()) {
                    customLists.forEach { list ->
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        ) {
                            Text(list.name, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            GlassButton(
                                text = "حذف القائمة",
                                onClick = {
                                    scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                        dao.clearListItems(list.listId)
                                        dao.deleteList(list.listId)
                                        withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "تم حذف قائمة ${list.name}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        }
                                    }
                                },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (hiddenIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "إظهار جميع القنوات المخفية (${hiddenIds.size})",
                        primary = true,
                        onClick = {
                            scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                dao.unhideAll(System.currentTimeMillis())
                                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "تمت استعادة جميع القنوات المخفية", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/settings/SettingsHomeChannelsPanel.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.settings

import android.content.Context
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column

import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.HomeChannelsLayout

import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.UserInfo
