@Composable
internal fun HomeChannelsSelectionPanel(
    container: AppContainer,
    source: HomeChannelsSource,
    selectedCategoryId: String,
    customChannelIds: Set<String>,
    onSelectSource: (HomeChannelsSource) -> Unit,
    onSelectCategory: (String) -> Unit,
    onToggleCustomChannel: (String) -> Unit,
    onClearCustomChannels: () -> Unit,
) {
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    var liveCategories by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Category>>(emptyList()) }
    var allChannels by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }
    var showChannelPickerDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(container.repo.account) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            runCatching {
                liveCategories = container.repo.liveCategories()
                allChannels = container.repo.peekAllLiveChannels() ?: container.repo.liveChannels(null)
            }
        }
    }

    GlassPanel(shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Tv, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text(
                        "اختيار قنوات الشاشة الرئيسية",
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                        color = Palette.Text,
                    )
                    Text(
                        "حدد مصدر القنوات التي تظهر في شريط القنوات المباشرة بالصفحة الرئيسية",
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = Palette.TextDim,
                    )
                }
            }

            // Option 1: AUTO
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "اختيار ذكي تلقائي (الافتراضي)",
                subtitle = "يعرض أشهر القنوات العربية والرياضية والإخبارية تلقائياً",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.AUTO,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_auto",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.AUTO) },
            )

            // Option 2: FAVORITES
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "قنواتي المفضلة فقط",
                subtitle = "يعرض القنوات المباشرة التي قمت بإضافتها إلى المفضلة",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.FAVORITES,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_favs",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.FAVORITES) },
            )

            // Option 3: CATEGORY
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "باقة محددة من السيرفر",
                subtitle = if (selectedCategoryId.isNotBlank()) {
                    val catName = liveCategories.firstOrNull { it.id == selectedCategoryId }?.name ?: selectedCategoryId
                    "الباقة المختارة: $catName"
                } else {
                    "اختر باقة كاملة (مثل beIN أو MBC أو OSN) لعرض قنواتها في الرئيسية"
                },
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_category",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY) },
            )

            if (source == HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY && liveCategories.isNotEmpty()) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.04f))
                        .padding(12.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                ) {
                    DotTitle("اختر الباقة المفضلة للرئيسية:", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 14.sp)
                    liveCategories.take(25).chunked(2).forEach { pair ->
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        ) {
                            pair.forEach { cat ->
                                val isCatSelected = cat.id == selectedCategoryId
                                GlassCard(
                                    onClick = { onSelectCategory(cat.id) },
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cat_pick_${cat.id}"),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                    selected = isCatSelected,
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = cat.name,
                                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                                        fontWeight = if (isCatSelected) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        color = if (isCatSelected) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            if (pair.size == 1) Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Option 4: CUSTOM
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "تحديد قنوات مخصصة يدوياً (${customChannelIds.size} قناة مختارة)",
                subtitle = "اختر القنوات التي تريدها بالاسم واحدة تلو الأخرى لوضعها في الصفحة الرئيسية",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_custom",
                onClick = {
                    onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                    focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_ch_source_custom")
                    showChannelPickerDialog = true
                },
            )

            Row(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تحديد القنوات يدوياً (${customChannelIds.size})",
                    onClick = {
                        onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                        focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn")
                        showChannelPickerDialog = true
                    },
                    primary = true,
                    icon = Ic.Tv,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn"),
                )
                if (customChannelIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "مسح الاختيار",
                        onClick = onClearCustomChannels,
                        modifier = Modifier.restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_custom_ch_btn"),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showChannelPickerDialog) {
        CustomChannelPickerDialog(
            allChannels = allChannels,
            selectedIds = customChannelIds,
            onToggleChannel = onToggleCustomChannel,
            onDismiss = {
                showChannelPickerDialog = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn")
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun CustomChannelPickerDialog(
    allChannels: List<Channel>,
    selectedIds: Set<String>,
    onToggleChannel: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    var query by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var filteredChannels by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }

    LaunchedEffect(allChannels, query, selectedIds) {
        if (query.isNotBlank()) {
            delay(180L)
        }
        val result = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val needle = query.normalizeForSearch().trim()
            if (needle.isBlank()) {
                val selectedFirst = allChannels.filter { it.id in selectedIds }
                val others = allChannels.filter { it.id !in selectedIds }.take(150)
                selectedFirst + others
            } else {
                allChannels.asSequence()
                    .filter { it.name.normalizeForSearch().contains(needle) }
                    .take(150)
                    .toList()
            }
        }
        filteredChannels = result
    }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(max = 520.dp),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 18.dp,
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            "اختر قنوات الشاشة الرئيسية",
                            fontSize = 17.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                            color = Color.White,
                        )
                        Text(
                            "تم تحديد ${selectedIds.size} قناة",
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            color = Palette.FocusCyan,
                        )
                    }
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "تم",
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        primary = true,
                    )
                }

                SearchBox(
                    value = query,
                    onValueChange = { query = it },
                    placeholder = "ابحث عن قناة (مثل: beIN, MBC, الجزيرة، روتانا)...",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )

                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 4.dp),
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(filteredChannels, key = { idx, ch -> "${ch.id}_$idx" }) { _, ch ->
                        val checked = ch.id in selectedIds
                        val logoUrl = remember(ch.icon, ch.name) {
                            ChannelLogoResolver.resolvePrimaryUrl(ch.icon, ch.name)
                        }
                        GlassCard(
                            onClick = { onToggleChannel(ch.id) },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                            selected = checked,
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                            ) {
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = checked,
                                    onCheckedChange = null,
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(
                                        checkedColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                                        uncheckedColor = Palette.TextDim,
                                        checkmarkColor = Palette.CinematicBg,
                                    ),
                                )
                                Box(
                                    Modifier
                                        .size(34.dp)
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(Color.White)
                                        .padding(4.dp),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                                ) {
                                    if (logoUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                                        AsyncImage(
                                            model = logoUrl,
                                            contentDescription = ch.name,
                                            contentScale = ContentScale.Fit,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        Text(
                                            text = ch.name.take(3),
                                            fontSize = 9.sp,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = Palette.CinematicBg,
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                                Text(
                                    text = ch.name,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (checked) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SourceOptionCard(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        selected = selected,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            RadioButton(
                selected = selected,
                onClick = null,
                colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(
                    selectedColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                    unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim,
                ),
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    title,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = if (selected) Color.White else Palette.Text,
                )
                Text(
                    subtitle,
                    fontSize = 11.5.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun HomeCustomizationPanel(
    showRecents: Boolean,
    showLiveChannels: Boolean,
    showRecommended: Boolean,
    showQuickCategories: Boolean,
    homeChannelsLayout: HomeChannelsLayout,
    onToggleShowRecents: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowLive: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowRecommended: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowQuickCategories: () -> Unit,
    onSelectLayout: (HomeChannelsLayout) -> Unit,
    onClearRecents: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Settings, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text("التحكم في أقسام الشاشة الرئيسية", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text("إظهار أو إخفاء أي قسم وتخصيص طريقة العرض", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                }
            }

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم تابع المشاهدة",
                subtitle = "إظهار شريط استكمال مشاهدة الأفلام والمسلسلات والقنوات",
                checked = showRecents,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_recents",
                onToggle = onToggleShowRecents,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم القنوات المباشرة",
                subtitle = "إظهار شريط القنوات المباشرة المميزة في الصفحة الرئيسية",
                checked = showLiveChannels,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_live_channels",
                onToggle = onToggleShowLive,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم أفلام مقترحة لك (أحدث الإضافات)",
                subtitle = "إظهار أحدث الأفلام المضافة من سيرفر Xtream وقاعدة بيانات Firebase",
                checked = showRecommended,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_recommended",
                onToggle = onToggleShowRecommended,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض بطاقات التصفح السريع",
                subtitle = "إظهار بطاقات البث المباشر — الأفلام — المسلسلات",
                checked = showQuickCategories,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_quick_cats",
                onToggle = onToggleShowQuickCategories,
            )

            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            DotTitle("طريقة عرض القنوات المباشرة في الرئيسية", Palette.Royal, fontSize = 15.sp)
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                val isCarousel = homeChannelsLayout == HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL
                GlassCard(
                    onClick = { onSelectLayout(HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL) },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_layout_carousel"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    selected = isCarousel,
                ) {
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isCarousel,
                            onClick = null,
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Palette.Glow, unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text("سلايدر أفقي", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isCarousel) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim)
                            Text("شريط متحرك", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                        }
                    }
                }
                val isGrid = homeChannelsLayout == HomeChannelsLayout.GRID
                GlassCard(
                    onClick = { onSelectLayout(HomeChannelsLayout.GRID) },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_layout_grid"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    selected = isGrid,
                ) {
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isGrid,
                            onClick = null,
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Palette.Glow, unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text("شبكة", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isGrid) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim)
                            Text("صفوف متعددة", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            GlassButton(
                text = "مسح سجل المشاهدة بالكامل",
                onClick = onClearRecents,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_recents_btn"),
            )
            val cacheCtx = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext.current
            val cacheScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
            var cacheCleared by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
            GlassButton(
                text = if (cacheCleared) "تم مسح كاش الصور والفيديو" else "مسح كاش الصور والفيديو",
                onClick = {
                    cacheScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player.SahabPlayer.clearMediaCache(cacheCtx)
                        runCatching { java.io.File(cacheCtx.cacheDir, "image_cache").deleteRecursively() }
                        withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { cacheCleared = true }
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_media_cache_btn"),
            )
            val weatherSvc = com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalContainer.current.weather
            var cityInput by remember { mutableStateOf(weatherSvc.getManualCity()) }
            var cityStatus by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                GlassTextField(
                    value = cityInput,
                    onValueChange = { cityInput = it; cityStatus = null },
                    placeholder = "مدينة الطقس (اتركه فارغاً للتلقائي)",
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                )
                GlassButton(
                    text = "حفظ",
                    onClick = {
                        cacheScope.launch {
                            val ok = weatherSvc.setManualCity(cityInput)
                            cityStatus = if (ok) {
                                if (cityInput.isBlank()) "تم التفعيل التلقائي" else "تم تثبيت المدينة"
                            } else "لم يتم العثور على المدينة"
                        }
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_save_weather_city_btn"),
                )
            }
            cityStatus?.let { st ->
                Text(st, fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.Gold)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SettingsSwitchRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onToggle,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {
                toggleableState = ToggleableState(checked)
                contentDescription = "$title، $subtitle"
            }
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        selected = checked,
        role = Role.Switch,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                DotTitle(title, if (checked) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 14.sp)
                Spacer(Modifier.height(3.dp))
                Text(subtitle, fontSize = 11.5.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
            }
            Switch(
                checked = checked,
                onCheckedChange = null,
                colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                    checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                    checkedTrackColor = Palette.Royal,
                    uncheckedThumbColor = Palette.TextDim,
                    uncheckedTrackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f),
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onLoggedOut: () -> Unit,
    onOpenCloudLogin: () -> Unit = {},
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val displayMode = LocalDisplayMode.current
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    val isWide = displayMode == DisplayMode.TABLET_OR_TV

    val savedCategory = focusNav.getSelectedCategory(Routes.SETTINGS)
    var selectedCategory by remember(isWide) {
        mutableStateOf<String?>(savedCategory ?: if (isWide) "appearance" else null)
    }
    var showLogoutConfirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val selectCat: (String?) -> Unit = { cat ->
        selectedCategory = cat
        if (cat != null) {
            focusNav.setSelectedCategory(Routes.SETTINGS, cat)
        }
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = !isWide && selectedCategory != null) {
        val prevCat = selectedCategory
        selectedCategory = null
        if (prevCat != null) {
            focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_menu_$prevCat")
        }
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = isWide) {
        val activeKey = "settings_menu_${selectedCategory ?: "appearance"}"
        focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, activeKey)
        onBack()
    }

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        if (isWide || selectedCategory == null) {
            SahabTopBar(current = Routes.SETTINGS, onBack = onBack)
        }

        if (isWide) {
            Row(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(22.dp),
            ) {
                // Right column in RTL: Settings Sidebar
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.32f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .focusGroup()
                        .focusRestorer()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .padding(bottom = 12.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                ) {
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المظهر وتصميم الشاشات",
                        subtitle = "تخطيط الأفلام، المسلسلات، الثيمات، والأداء",
                        icon = Ic.Settings,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "appearance",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_appearance",
                        isDefault = true,
                        onClick = { selectCat("appearance") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "قوائم البث والتخصيص",
                        subtitle = "قنوات الرئيسية، القوائم المخصصة، والمخفية",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "custom_tv",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_custom_tv",
                        onClick = { selectCat("custom_tv") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المصادر والاشتراكات",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مصادر Xtream / M3U وتحديث الاشتراك",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "sources",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_sources",
                        onClick = { selectCat("sources") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الحساب السحابي والمزامنة",
                        subtitle = "تسجيل الدخول، رمز التشفير، والمزامنة",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "cloud",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_cloud",
                        onClick = { selectCat("cloud") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الذكاء الاصطناعي (Gemini)",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مفاتيح توليد قصص الأفلام",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "ai",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_ai",
                        onClick = { selectCat("ai") },
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = {
                            focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn")
                            showLogoutConfirm = true
                        },
                        icon = Ic.Close,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn"),
                    )
                }

                // Left column in RTL: Active Settings Panel
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.68f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .focusGroup()
                        .focusRestorer()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                ) {
                    SettingsDetailContent(selectedCategory ?: "appearance", container, onOpenCloudLogin, onLoggedOut)
                }
            }
        } else {
            if (selectedCategory == null) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                ) {
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المظهر وتصميم الشاشات",
                        subtitle = "تخطيط الأفلام، المسلسلات، الثيمات، والأداء",
                        icon = Ic.Settings,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_appearance",
                        onClick = { selectCat("appearance") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "قوائم البث والتخصيص",
                        subtitle = "قنوات الرئيسية، القوائم المخصصة، والمخفية",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_custom_tv",
                        onClick = { selectCat("custom_tv") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المصادر والاشتراكات",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مصادر Xtream / M3U وتحديث الاشتراك",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_sources",
                        onClick = { selectCat("sources") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الحساب السحابي والمزامنة",
                        subtitle = "تسجيل الدخول، رمز التشفير، والمزامنة",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_cloud",
                        onClick = { selectCat("cloud") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الذكاء الاصطناعي (Gemini)",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مفاتيح توليد قصص الأفلام",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_ai",
                        onClick = { selectCat("ai") },
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = { showLogoutConfirm = true },
                        icon = Ic.Close,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )

                    Text(
                        text = Ar.version(appVersion(context).ifBlank { "1.0" }),
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        color = Palette.TextDim,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                    )
                }
            } else {
                Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    SahabTopBar(
                        current = Routes.SETTINGS,
                        onBack = {
                            val prev = selectedCategory
                            selectedCategory = null
                            if (prev != null) {
                                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_menu_$prev")
                            }
                        },
                    )
                    Column(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                            .padding(16.dp),
                    ) {
                        SettingsDetailContent(selectedCategory, container, onOpenCloudLogin, onLoggedOut)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showLogoutConfirm) {
        LogoutConfirmationDialog(
            onConfirm = {
                showLogoutConfirm = false
                onLoggedOut()
            },
            onDismiss = {
                showLogoutConfirm = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn")
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LogoutConfirmationDialog(
    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 22.dp,
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                Text(Ar.logoutQuestion, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                Text(Ar.logoutHint, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = onConfirm,
                        primary = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.cancel,
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        autoFocus = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsMenuItem(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    icon: ImageVector,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    isDefault: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey, isDefaultEntry = isDefault)
            .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
        selected = isSelected || focused,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .size(44.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .background(if (isSelected || focused) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.4f) else Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                    .border(1.dp, Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
            ) {
                Icon(icon, null, tint = Palette.FocusCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(title, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                Text(
                    subtitle,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
            }
            Text("<", fontSize = 20.sp, color = Palette.Glow, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsDetailContent(
    category: String?,
    container: AppContainer,
    onOpenCloudLogin: () -> Unit,
    onLoggedOut: () -> Unit,
) {
    val cloudUser = container.authManager.currentUser
    val account: Account? = container.repo.account
    val info: UserInfo? = container.repo.userInfo

    val homeChannelsSource by container.prefs.homeChannelsSource.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = HomeChannelsSource.AUTO)
    val homeChannelsCategoryId by container.prefs.homeChannelsCategoryId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = "")
    val homeCustomChannelIds by container.prefs.homeCustomChannelIds.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptySet())

    when (category) {
        "appearance", "customize", "themes" -> SettingsAppearancePanel(container = container)
        "custom_tv", "home_channels" -> SettingsCustomTvPanel(
            container = container,
            homeChannelsSource = homeChannelsSource,
            homeChannelsCategoryId = homeChannelsCategoryId,
            homeCustomChannelIds = homeCustomChannelIds,
        )
        "cloud" -> {
            CloudAccountPanel(
                email = cloudUser?.email,
                onLogin = onOpenCloudLogin,
                onLogout = {
                    container.authManager.signOut()
                    onLoggedOut()
                }
            )
        }
        "sources" -> {
            val appCtx = LocalContext.current.applicationContext
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
                SourcesPanel(container = container)
                ActiveSourceDetailsPanel(
                    container = container,
                    account = account,
                    info = info,
                    onSyncNow = {
                        container.syncCoordinator.triggerManualSync(appCtx)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
        "ai" -> {
            AiKeysPanel(container.aiKeys)
        }
    }
}

internal fun hostOf(server: String): String = server.substringAfter("://").trimEnd('/')

@Composable
internal fun InfoRow(label: String, value: String) {
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 6.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Text(label, fontSize = 14.sp, color = Palette.TextDim, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
        Text(value, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
    }
}



@Composable
internal fun SourcesPanel(container: AppContainer) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val activeAccount by container.prefs.account.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = null)
    val isSwitching by container.switchingAccount.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var accounts by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Account>>(emptyList()) }
    var showAddDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(activeAccount) {
        accounts = container.prefs.getAccounts()
    }

    GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(Modifier.size(44.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f)).border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Icon(Ic.Tv, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text("المصادر والاشتراكات", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                        Text("اختر المصدر النشط أو أضف مصدراً جديداً", fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                    }
                }
                GlassButton(
                    text = "+ إضافة",
                    onClick = { showAddDialog = true },
                    primary = true,
                    icon = Ic.Cloud
                )
            }

            accounts.forEach { acc ->
                val isActive = acc.id == activeAccount?.id
                GlassPanel(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .then(
                            if (isActive) Modifier.border(1.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.7f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            else Modifier,
                        ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    padding = 16.dp,
                ) {
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(acc.name, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                            Text(if (acc.type == "M3U") "رابط M3U مباشر" else acc.server, fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                        }
                        
                        if (!isActive) {
                            if (isSwitching) {
                                CircularProgressIndicator(color = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                            } else {
                                GlassButton(text = "تفعيل", onClick = {
                                    if (!isSwitching) {
                                        container.switchAccount(acc)
                                    }
                                })
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        } else {
                            Tag("نشط", Palette.Emerald)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        }
                        
                        GlassButton(text = "حذف", onClick = {
                            scope.launch {
                                container.prefs.deleteAccount(acc.id)
                                val newList = container.prefs.getAccounts()
                                accounts = newList
                                container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(newList)
                                if (acc.id == activeAccount?.id) {
                                    val next = newList.firstOrNull()
                                    if (next != null) {
                                        container.switchAccount(next)
                                    } else {
                                        container.clearAllLocalData(clearPrefsAccount = true)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        })
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showAddDialog) {
        AddSourceDialog(
            container = container,
            onDismiss = { showAddDialog = false },
            onAdded = { newAcc ->
                scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    accounts = container.prefs.getAccounts()
                    container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(accounts)
                    container.switchAccount(newAcc)
                }
                showAddDialog = false
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun AddSourceDialog(container: AppContainer, onDismiss: () -> Unit, onAdded: (Account) -> Unit) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var selectedTab by remember { mutableStateOf("XTREAM") }
    var name by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var server by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var user by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var pass by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var m3uUrl by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var loading by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var errorMsg by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 22.dp) {
            Column(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                    .imePadding(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
            ) {
                Text("إضافة مصدر جديد", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    GlassCard(onClick = { selectedTab = "XTREAM" }, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), selected = selectedTab == "XTREAM") {
                        Text("سيرفر Xtream", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(12.dp), textAlign = TextAlign.Center, color = if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") Color.White else Palette.TextDim)
                    }
                    GlassCard(onClick = { selectedTab = "M3U" }, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), selected = selectedTab == "M3U") {
                        Text("رابط M3U", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(12.dp), textAlign = TextAlign.Center, color = if (selectedTab == "M3U") Color.White else Palette.TextDim)
                    }
                }

                GlassTextField(value = name, onValueChange = { name = it }, placeholder = "اسم المصدر (مثال: اشتراكي)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                
                if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") {
                    GlassTextField(value = server, onValueChange = { server = it }, placeholder = "عنوان السيرفر (http://...)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    GlassTextField(value = user, onValueChange = { user = it }, placeholder = "اسم المستخدم", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    GlassTextField(value = pass, onValueChange = { pass = it }, placeholder = "كلمة المرور", password = true, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                } else {
                    GlassTextField(value = m3uUrl, onValueChange = { m3uUrl = it }, placeholder = "رابط M3U المباشر (http://...)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                }
                val activeUrl = (if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") server else m3uUrl).trim()
                if (activeUrl.isNotEmpty() && !activeUrl.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true)) {
                    HttpSecurityWarningBanner()
                }
                errorMsg?.let { msg ->
                    Text(msg, fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.Gold)
                }

                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = if (loading) "جاري الفحص..." else "حفظ",
                        onClick = {
                            if (loading) return@GlassButton
                            val normalizedServer = if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") {
                                com.saydlawy.sahab.util.ServerUrl.normalizeServer(server)
                            } else null
                            val validUrl = if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") {
                                normalizedServer != null && user.isNotBlank() && pass.isNotBlank()
                            } else {
                                m3uUrl.trim().startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                    m3uUrl.trim().startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true)
                            }
                            if (!validUrl) {
                                errorMsg = "تأكد من إدخال رابط صحيح مع كامل البيانات."
                                return@GlassButton
                            }
                            errorMsg = null
                            loading = true
                            scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                try {
                                    val acc = if (selectedTab == "XTREAM") {
                                        Account(name = name.ifBlank { "Xtream Server" }, type = "XTREAM", server = normalizedServer!!, user = user.trim(), pass = pass)
                                    } else {
                                        Account(name = name.ifBlank { "M3U List" }, type = "M3U", m3uUrl = m3uUrl.trim())
                                    }
                                    container.repo.login(acc)
                                    container.prefs.saveAccount(acc)
                                    withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                        loading = false
                                        onAdded(acc)
                                    }
                                } catch (e: Exception) {
                                    withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                        loading = false
                                        errorMsg = e.message?.take(120) ?: "تعذر الاتصال بالمصدر، تأكد من الرابط والبيانات."
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        primary = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    GlassButton(text = "إلغاء", onClick = onDismiss, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun ActiveSourceDetailsPanel(
    container: AppContainer,
    account: Account?,
    info: UserInfo?,
    onSyncNow: () -> Unit,
) {
    if (account == null) return
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var liveInfo by remember(account.id, info) { mutableStateOf(info) }
    var refreshingInfo by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier.size(44.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f)).border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Person, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text("تفاصيل المصدر النشط", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text(if (account.type == "M3U") "رابط M3U" else account.server, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.Glow)
                }
            }
            if (account.type == "XTREAM" && liveInfo != null) {
                Tag(liveInfo?.status.orEmpty().ifBlank { "Active" }, Palette.Emerald)
            }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
        
        if (account.type == "XTREAM") {
            InfoRow("الخادم", hostOf(account.server))
            InfoRow("اسم المستخدم", account.user)
            liveInfo?.let { currentInfo ->
                InfoRow("الحالة", if (currentInfo.status.isBlank()) "Active" else currentInfo.status)
                val expiry = currentInfo.expiry
                InfoRow("ينتهي الاشتراك", if (expiry == null || expiry <= 0L) Ar.subscriptionOpenShort else formatDate(expiry))
                if (currentInfo.maxConnections.isNotBlank()) {
                    val maxText = if (currentInfo.maxConnections == "0") "غير محدود" else currentInfo.maxConnections
                    InfoRow("الأجهزة المتصلة", "${currentInfo.activeConnections.ifBlank { "0" }} / $maxText")
                }
            }
        } else {
            InfoRow("الرابط", account.m3uUrl.take(40) + "...")
        }
        
        Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            if (account.type == "XTREAM") {
                GlassButton(
                    text = if (refreshingInfo) "جاري تحديث الاشتراك..." else "تحديث بيانات الاشتراك",
                    onClick = {
                        if (refreshingInfo) return@GlassButton
                        refreshingInfo = true
                        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                            val result = runCatching { container.repo.refreshUserInfo() }
                            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                refreshingInfo = false
                                result.onSuccess { updated ->
                                    liveInfo = updated
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "تم تحديث بيانات الاشتراك", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }.onFailure { err ->
                                    Toast.makeText(context, err.message ?: "تعذر تحديث بيانات الاشتراك", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    icon = Ic.Person,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )
            }
            GlassButton(
                text = "تحديث ومزامنة القنوات الآن",
                onClick = onSyncNow,
                icon = Ic.Refresh,
                primary = true,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color

data class OledAccentPreset(
    val id: String,
    val nameAr: String,
    val accent: Color,
    val focus: Color,
    val glow: Color,
)

val OledAccentPresets: List<OledAccentPreset> = listOf(
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_cyan",
        nameAr = "أزرق سماوي",
        accent = Color(0xFF0EA5E9),
        focus = Color(0xFF38BDF8),
        glow = Color(0xFF7DD3FC),
    ),
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_prime",
        nameAr = "أزرق برايم",
        accent = Color(0xFF00A8E1),
        focus = Color(0xFF00C2FF),
        glow = Color(0xFF38BDF8),
    ),
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_netflix",
        nameAr = "أحمر سينمائي",
        accent = Color(0xFFE50914),
        focus = Color(0xFFFF3B30),
        glow = Color(0xFFFB7185),
    ),
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_gold",
        nameAr = "ذهبي فاخر",
        accent = Color(0xFFE0A23B),
        focus = Color(0xFFF59E0B),
        glow = Color(0xFFFBBF24),
    ),
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_emerald",
        nameAr = "زمردي",
        accent = Color(0xFF10B981),
        focus = Color(0xFF34D399),
        glow = Color(0xFF6EE7B7),
    ),
    OledAccentPreset(
        id = "oled_violet",
        nameAr = "بنفسجي ملكي",
        accent = Color(0xFF8E45FF),
        focus = Color(0xFFA855F7),
        glow = Color(0xFFC084FC),
    ),
)

fun oledAccentPresetFor(id: String): OledAccentPreset =
    OledAccentPresets.firstOrNull { it.id == id } ?: OledAccentPresets.first()

fun applyOledCinemaTokens(base: MutableMap<String, Color>, accentPresetId: String) {
    val preset = oledAccentPresetFor(accentPresetId)
    base["DeepBlue"] = Color.Black
    base["CinematicBg"] = Color.Black
    base["CinematicBgSecondary"] = Color(0xFF050507)
    base["BgTop"] = Color.Black
    base["CardSurface"] = Color(0xFF0A0A0D)
    base["CardSurfaceElevated"] = Color(0xFF14141A)
    base["CardBorderIdle"] = Color(0xFF1F1F29)
    base["Royal"] = preset.accent
    base["Blue"] = preset.glow
    base["FocusCyan"] = preset.focus
    base["FocusBlueGlow"] = preset.focus
    base["Glow"] = preset.glow
    base["Glass"] = Color(0xE60A0A0D)
    base["GlassStrong"] = Color(0xF50A0A0D)
    base["GlassBorder"] = Color(0xFF1F1F29)
    base["InputBg"] = Color(0xFF0A0A0D)
    base["DialogBg"] = Color(0xF50A0A0D)
    base["ProgressStart"] = preset.focus
    base["OsdSurfaceLowest"] = Color.Black
    base["OsdSurfaceLow"] = Color(0xFF0A0A0D)
    base["OsdSurfaceContainer"] = Color(0xFF0A0A0D)
    base["OsdSurfaceHigh"] = Color(0xFF14141A)
    base["OsdSurfaceHighest"] = Color(0xFF14141A)
    base["OsdPrimaryContainer"] = preset.accent
    base["OsdCyan"] = preset.focus
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme

private fun colorMapForTheme(themeId: String, oledAccentPreset: String = "oled_cyan"): Map<String, Color> {
    val cosmic = mutableMapOf(
        "DeepBlue" to Color(0xFF0B0F24), "TextMuted" to Color(0xFF94A3B8),
        "CinematicBg" to Color(0xFF0B0D17), "CinematicBgSecondary" to Color(0xFF101322),
        "CardSurface" to Color(0xFF161A29), "CardSurfaceElevated" to Color(0xFF1E2336),
        "CardBorderIdle" to Color(0x26FFFFFF), "FocusCyan" to Color(0xFF38BDF8),
        "FocusBlueGlow" to Color(0xFF0EA5E9), "LiveBadgeRed" to Color(0xFFE53935),
        "NewBadgeBlue" to Color(0xFF2563EB), "CategorySeriesStart" to Color(0xFF5B21B6),
        "CategorySeriesEnd" to Color(0xFF2E1065), "CategoryMoviesStart" to Color(0xFF991B1B),
        "CategoryMoviesEnd" to Color(0xFF450A0A), "CategoryLiveStart" to Color(0xFF0E7490),
        "CategoryLiveEnd" to Color(0xFF083344), "Royal" to Color(0xFF8E45FF),
        "Glow" to Color(0xFF7DC4FF), "Gold" to Color(0xFFE0A23B),
        "Blue" to Color(0xFF2563EB), "Emerald" to Color(0xFF34D399),
        "Glass" to Color(0x8C12122D), "GlassStrong" to Color(0xA612122D),
        "GlassBorder" to Color(0x14FFFFFF), "InputBg" to Color(0xB30F0B1E),
        "BgTop" to Color(0xFF111433), "Text" to Color.White, "TextDim" to Color(0xFFA9AEC7),
        "DialogBg" to Color(0xF212122D), "Danger" to Color(0xFFFF6B6B),
        "ProgressStart" to Color(0xFF60A5FA),
        "OsdSurfaceLowest" to Color(0xFF0F0B1E), "OsdSurfaceLow" to Color(0xFF1D192C),
        "OsdSurfaceContainer" to Color(0xFF211D31), "OsdSurfaceHigh" to Color(0xFF2B273C),
        "OsdSurfaceHighest" to Color(0xFF363247), "OsdPrimary" to Color(0xFFDCB8FF),
        "OsdPrimaryContainer" to Color(0xFF8A2BE2), "OsdOnPrimary" to Color(0xFF480081),
        "OsdCyan" to Color(0xFF00FBFB), "OsdTertiary" to Color(0xFFC4C1FB),
        "OsdOnSurface" to Color(0xFFE7DEFA), "OsdOnSurfaceVariant" to Color(0xFFCFC2D7),
        "OsdOutline" to Color(0xFF988CA0), "OsdOutlineVariant" to Color(0xFF4C4354),
    )
    when (themeId) {
        "cosmic_glass_pro" -> {
            cosmic["Glass"] = Color(0x9912122D)
            cosmic["GlassStrong"] = Color(0xCC12122D)
            cosmic["GlassBorder"] = Color(0x26FFFFFF)
        }
        "oled_cinema" -> applyOledCinemaTokens(cosmic, oledAccentPreset)
        "dynamic_bento" -> {
            cosmic["Glass"] = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
            cosmic["GlassStrong"] = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
            cosmic["GlassBorder"] = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f)
        }
    }
    return cosmic
}

fun themeBaseColor(themeId: String, token: String, oledAccentPreset: String = "oled_cyan"): Color =
    colorMapForTheme(themeId, oledAccentPreset)[token] ?: Color.Magenta

private fun parseThemeColor(value: String): Color? {
    val hex = value.trim().removePrefix("#")
    val parsed = hex.toLongOrNull(16) ?: return null
    return when (hex.length) {
        6 -> Color((0xFF000000L or parsed).toInt())
        8 -> Color(parsed.toInt())
        else -> null
    }
}

private fun resolvedColors(settings: ThemePreferences): Map<String, Color> {
    val id = settings.selectedThemeId
    val base = colorMapForTheme(id, settings.oledAccentPreset).toMutableMap()
    settings.customization(id).colorOverrides.forEach { (key, value) ->
        parseThemeColor(value)?.let { color -> if (key in base) base[key] = color }
    }
    settings.customization(id).let { customization ->
        val alpha = customization.glassOpacity.coerceIn(0.15f, 1f)
        listOf("Glass", "GlassStrong").forEach { key ->
            base[key]?.let { color -> base[key] = color.copy(alpha = color.alpha * alpha) }
        }
    }
    return base
}

/** Dynamic palette backed by persisted theme state; every screen observes the same tokens. */
object Palette {
    private var colors by mutableStateOf(colorMapForTheme("cosmic_glass"))
    internal fun install(resolved: Map<String, Color>) { colors = resolved }
    internal fun install(settings: ThemePreferences) { colors = resolvedColors(settings) }
    fun color(token: String): Color = colors[token] ?: Color.Magenta
    private fun token(name: String) = color(name)

    val DeepBlue get() = token("DeepBlue")
    val TextMuted get() = token("TextMuted")
    val CinematicBg get() = token("CinematicBg")
    val CinematicBgSecondary get() = token("CinematicBgSecondary")
    val CardSurface get() = token("CardSurface")
    val CardSurfaceElevated get() = token("CardSurfaceElevated")
    val CardBorderIdle get() = token("CardBorderIdle")
    val CardBorder get() = CardBorderIdle
    val FocusCyan get() = token("FocusCyan")
    val FocusBlueGlow get() = token("FocusBlueGlow")
    val LiveBadgeRed get() = token("LiveBadgeRed")
    val LiveRed get() = LiveBadgeRed
    val NewBadgeBlue get() = token("NewBadgeBlue")
    val CategorySeriesStart get() = token("CategorySeriesStart")
    val CategorySeriesEnd get() = token("CategorySeriesEnd")
    val CategoryMoviesStart get() = token("CategoryMoviesStart")
    val CategoryMoviesEnd get() = token("CategoryMoviesEnd")
    val CategoryLiveStart get() = token("CategoryLiveStart")
    val CategoryLiveEnd get() = token("CategoryLiveEnd")
    val Royal get() = token("Royal")
    val Glow get() = token("Glow")
    val Live get() = token("LiveBadgeRed")
    val Gold get() = token("Gold")
    val Blue get() = token("Blue")
    val Emerald get() = token("Emerald")
    val Glass get() = token("Glass")
    val GlassStrong get() = token("GlassStrong")
    val GlassBorder get() = token("GlassBorder")
    val InputBg get() = token("InputBg")
    val Bg get() = DeepBlue
    val BgTop get() = token("BgTop")
    val Violet get() = Royal
    val Pink get() = Glow
    val Cyan get() = Glow
    val Peach get() = Gold
    val Text get() = token("Text")
    val TextDim get() = token("TextDim")
    val DialogBg get() = token("DialogBg")
    val Danger get() = token("Danger")
    val Accent get() = Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(Royal, Blue, Royal))
    val ActiveFill get() = Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(Royal.copy(alpha = 0.45f), DeepBlue.copy(alpha = 0.85f)))
    val Progress get() = Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(token("ProgressStart"), Royal))
    val BrandText get() = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Color.White, Glow, Royal))
    val Logo get() = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Royal, Blue))
    val LogoBright get() = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Royal, Blue, Glow))
}

/**
 * Google Font "Cairo" (static TrueType instances bundled in res/font, so it works offline,
 * on Android TV boxes without Google Play Services, and on API 23+).
 */
val CairoFamily = FontFamily(
    Font(R.font.cairo_regular, FontWeight.Normal),
    Font(R.font.cairo_medium, FontWeight.Medium),
    Font(R.font.cairo_semibold, FontWeight.SemiBold),
    Font(R.font.cairo_bold, FontWeight.Bold),
    Font(R.font.cairo_extrabold, FontWeight.ExtraBold),
)

@Composable
fun SahabTheme(
    settings: ThemePreferences = ThemePreferences(),
    content: @Composable () -> Unit,
) {
    val customization = remember(settings) { settings.customization() }
    val colors = remember(settings) {
        val resolved = resolvedColors(settings)
        Palette.install(resolved)
        resolved
    }
    val sourceDensity = LocalDensity.current
    val tvFontBoost = if (com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalIsTv.current) 1.06f else 1f
    val scaledDensity = remember(sourceDensity, customization.fontScale, tvFontBoost) {
        Density(density = sourceDensity.density, fontScale = (sourceDensity.fontScale * customization.fontScale * tvFontBoost).coerceIn(0.7f, 1.65f))
    }
    val selectedFont = remember(customization.fontFamily) {
        when (customization.fontFamily) {
            "sans" -> FontFamily.SansSerif
            "serif" -> FontFamily.Serif
            "mono" -> FontFamily.Monospace
            else -> CairoFamily
        }
    }
    val scheme = darkColorScheme(
        primary = colors["Royal"] ?: Color(0xFF8E45FF),
        secondary = colors["Glow"] ?: Color(0xFF7DC4FF),
        background = colors["DeepBlue"] ?: Color(0xFF0B0F24),
        surface = colors["CardSurface"] ?: Color(0xFF161A29),
        onSurface = colors["Text"] ?: Color.White,
        onBackground = colors["Text"] ?: Color.White,
    )
    val base = Typography()
    val typography = base.copy(
        displayLarge = base.displayLarge.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        displayMedium = base.displayMedium.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        displaySmall = base.displaySmall.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        headlineLarge = base.headlineLarge.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        headlineMedium = base.headlineMedium.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        headlineSmall = base.headlineSmall.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        titleLarge = base.titleLarge.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        titleMedium = base.titleMedium.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        titleSmall = base.titleSmall.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        bodyLarge = base.bodyLarge.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        bodyMedium = base.bodyMedium.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        bodySmall = base.bodySmall.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        labelLarge = base.labelLarge.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        labelMedium = base.labelMedium.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
        labelSmall = base.labelSmall.copy(fontFamily = selectedFont),
    )
    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalSahabColors provides colors,
        LocalThemePreferences provides settings,
        LocalThemeCustomization provides customization,
        LocalDensity provides scaledDensity,
    ) {
        MaterialTheme(colorScheme = scheme, typography = typography) {
            CompositionLocalProvider(
                LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Rtl,
                LocalTextStyle provides TextStyle(
                    fontFamily = selectedFont,
                    color = colors["Text"] ?: Color.White,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                ),
                LocalContentColor provides (colors["Text"] ?: Color.White),
            ) {
                content()
            }
        }
    }
}

val LocalSahabColors = compositionLocalOf { colorMapForTheme("cosmic_glass") }
val LocalThemePreferences = compositionLocalOf { ThemePreferences() }
val LocalThemeCustomization = compositionLocalOf { ThemeCustomization() }

fun ThemeCustomization.isVisible(componentId: String): Boolean = when (componentId) {
    "global_navigation", "details_actions", "player_controls" -> true
    else -> componentVisibility[componentId] ?: true
}
fun ThemeCustomization.placement(componentId: String, fallback: String = "end"): String = componentPlacement[componentId] ?: fallback

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/util/ChannelLogoResolver.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.util

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * Resolves high-availability internet channel logos for Arabic & International IPTV channels
 * whenever the Xtream server provides an empty, broken, or unreachable `stream_icon`.
 */
object ChannelLogoResolver {

    private const val TV_LOGOS_BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tv-logo/tv-logos/main/countries"
    private const val WIKI_COMMONS = "https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons"

    data class ChannelBrandInfo(
        val logoUrl: String,
        val accentColor: Color,
        val badgeLabel: String,
    )

    /**
     * Returns the best primary URL to try first (either the server icon if valid HTTP/HTTPS,
     * or the resolved internet logo URL if the server icon is blank).
     */
    fun resolvePrimaryUrl(rawIcon: String, channelName: String): String {
        val trimmed = rawIcon.trim()
        if (trimmed.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true) ||
            trimmed.startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true)
        ) {
            return trimmed
        }
        return resolveInternetLogoUrl(channelName).orEmpty()
    }

    /**
     * Returns a reliable internet fallback logo URL based on the channel's Arabic or English name.
     * Used both when `channel.icon` is blank and when `channel.icon` fails to load from the IPTV server.
     */
    fun resolveInternetLogoUrl(channelName: String): String? {
        val n = normalizeChannelName(channelName)
        if (n.isBlank()) return null

        return when {
            // --- beIN SPORTS ---
            n.contains("bein") && (n.contains("news") || n.contains("اخبار") || n.contains("إخبار")) ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-news-me.png"
            n.contains("bein") && n.contains("max") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/france/bein-sports-max-4-fr.png"
            n.contains("bein") && n.contains("premium 1") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-1-premium-me.png"
            n.contains("bein") && n.contains("premium 2") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-2-premium-me.png"
            n.contains("bein") && n.contains("premium 3") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-3-premium-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("2") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-2-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("3") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-3-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("4") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-4-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("5") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-5-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("6") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-6-me.png"
            (n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت")) && n.contains("7") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-7-me.png"
            n.contains("bein") || n.contains("بي ان") || n.contains("بين سبورت") ->
                "$TV_LOGOS_BASE/middle-east/bein-sports-1-me.png"

            // --- SSC & ARAB SPORTS ---
            n.contains("ssc") || n.contains("اس اس سي") ->
                "$WIKI_COMMONS/thumb/c/c8/SSC_Sports_logo.svg/512px-SSC_Sports_logo.svg.png"
